<!-- ============================================================
     STICKY BUY CTA (sitewide)
     Paste into: Settings → Advanced → Code Injection → FOOTER, below the analytics layer.
     Appears after the visitor scrolls past the first section (the hero on the homepage).
     Mobile: full-width bar pinned to the bottom. Desktop: card pinned bottom-right.
     Hides itself while the main buy section or the page's final CTA band is on screen,
     and stays dismissed for the rest of the browser session once closed.
     Edit the values in RTT_STICKY below to change the text, and REACT for the
     lines Rally says when a page turns in the homepage Look Inside book.
     ============================================================ -->
<style>
  .rtt-sticky {
    --ink: #2b2622; --cream: #fbf5ea; --coral: #ee7b6c; --coral-dark: #e0665b; --coral-soft: #f8b3a8;
    position: fixed; left: 0; right: 0; bottom: 0; z-index: 9000;
    display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 12px;
    padding: 10px 14px calc(10px + env(safe-area-inset-bottom, 0px));
    background: var(--ink); color: var(--cream);

  }
  .rtt-sticky.is-on { transform: translateY(0); visibility: visible; transition-delay: 0s; }
  @keyframes rtt-wag { 0%,100% { transform: rotate(0); } 20% { transform: rotate(-8deg); } 40% { transform: rotate(7deg); } 60% { transform: rotate(-5deg); } 80% { transform: rotate(3deg); } }
  .rtt-sticky.is-on .rtt-sticky__img { animation: rtt-wag 900ms ease 350ms 1; transform-origin: 50% 90%; }
  .rtt-sticky *, .rtt-sticky *::before, .rtt-sticky *::after { box-sizing: border-box; }
  .rtt-sticky__img { flex: none; width: 46px; height: 46px; border-radius: 50%; object-fit: cover; border: 2px solid var(--coral); box-shadow: 0 6px 14px -6px rgba(0,0,0,.8); background: #fff; }
  .rtt-sticky__text { position: relative; flex: 1; min-width: 0; line-height: 1.25; }
  .rtt-sticky__stars { display: none; align-items: center; gap: 5px; margin-top: 3px; font-size: 12px; font-weight: 700; color: rgba(251,245,234,.72); }

  .rtt-sticky__btn {
    flex: none; display: inline-flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; gap: 8px; min-height: 44px; padding: 0 18px;
    border-radius: 999px; background: var(--coral); color: var(--ink) !important; font-weight: 800; font-size: 15px; letter-spacing: .01em; text-decoration: none !important;
    box-shadow: inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255,255,255,.35), 0 6px 14px -8px rgba(0,0,0,.6); transition: background 160ms ease, transform 120ms ease;
  }
  .rtt-sticky__btn:hover { background: var(--coral-dark); }
  .rtt-sticky__btn:active { transform: scale(.98); }
  .rtt-sticky__btn svg { width: 17px; height: 17px; flex: none; }
  .rtt-sticky__btn:focus-visible, .rtt-sticky__x:focus-visible { outline: 3px solid var(--cream); outline-offset: 2px; }
  .rtt-sticky__x {
    flex: none; width: 36px; height: 36px; display: grid; place-items: center; border: 0; border-radius: 50%;
    background: rgba(251,245,234,.1); color: var(--cream); cursor: pointer; font: inherit;
  }
  .rtt-sticky__x:hover { background: rgba(251,245,234,.2); }
  .rtt-sticky__x svg { width: 16px; height: 16px; }
  .rtt-sticky__sr { position: absolute; width: 1px; height: 1px; overflow: hidden; clip: rect(0 0 0 0); }
  body.rtt-sticky-pad { padding-bottom: 76px !important; }

  @media (min-width: 860px) {
    .rtt-sticky {
      left: auto; right: 24px; bottom: var(--rtt-sticky-bottom, 24px); width: 340px;
      display: grid; grid-template-columns: 56px minmax(0, 1fr); grid-template-rows: auto auto; gap: 6px 14px; align-items: center;
      padding: 16px 16px 16px; border-radius: 20px;
      box-shadow: 0 30px 60px -24px rgba(0,0,0,.65), 0 0 0 1px rgba(251,245,234,.08);
      transform: translateY(24px); opacity: 0; visibility: hidden; transition: transform 260ms ease, opacity 260ms ease, visibility 0s linear 260ms;
    }
    .rtt-sticky.is-on { transform: translateY(0); opacity: 1; visibility: visible; transition-delay: 0s; }
    .rtt-sticky__img { width: 56px; height: 56px; grid-row: 1; }
    .rtt-sticky__text { grid-row: 1; margin-right: 34px; }
    .rtt-sticky__title { font-size: 17px; }
    .rtt-sticky__deal { font-size: 13px; }
    .rtt-sticky__text { padding: 9px 12px 9px 14px; border-radius: 12px; background: rgba(251,245,234,.09); }
    .rtt-sticky__text::before { content: ""; position: absolute; left: -6px; top: 50%; width: 12px; height: 12px; background: rgba(251,245,234,.09); transform: translateY(-50%) rotate(45deg); border-radius: 2px; }
    .rtt-sticky__stars { display: inline-flex; }
    .rtt-sticky__btn { grid-column: 1 / -1; grid-row: 2; width: 100%; margin-top: 8px; min-height: 48px; font-size: 16px; }
    .rtt-sticky__x { position: absolute; top: 12px; right: 12px; width: 30px; height: 30px; }
    .rtt-sticky__x svg { width: 14px; height: 14px; }
    body.rtt-sticky-pad { padding-bottom: 0 !important; }
  }
  @media (max-width: 480px) { .rtt-sticky { gap: 10px; padding-left: 10px; padding-right: 10px; } .rtt-sticky__img { width: 40px; height: 40px; } .rtt-sticky__btn { padding: 0 14px; font-size: 14px; } .rtt-sticky__title { font-size: 13.5px; } .rtt-sticky__deal { font-size: 12px; } }
  /* reactions: when a page turns in the Look Inside book, Rally wags and the bubble pops */
  @keyframes rtt-wag-big { 0%,100% { transform: rotate(0) scale(1); } 15% { transform: rotate(-12deg) scale(1.08); } 35% { transform: rotate(10deg) scale(1.1); } 55% { transform: rotate(-8deg) scale(1.06); } 75% { transform: rotate(5deg) scale(1.03); } }
  @keyframes rtt-pop { 0% { transform: scale(.92); opacity: .4; } 60% { transform: scale(1.04); opacity: 1; } 100% { transform: scale(1); opacity: 1; } }
  .rtt-sticky.is-talking .rtt-sticky__img { animation: rtt-wag-big 820ms cubic-bezier(.3,.7,.3,1) 1; transform-origin: 50% 90%; }
  .rtt-sticky.is-talking .rtt-sticky__text { animation: rtt-pop 420ms cubic-bezier(.2,.8,.3,1.2) 1; transform-origin: 0 50%; }
  @media (max-width: 859px) { .rtt-sticky.is-talking .rtt-sticky__text { transform-origin: 0 50%; } }
  @media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) { .rtt-sticky, .rtt-sticky__btn { transition: none; } .rtt-sticky.is-on .rtt-sticky__img, .rtt-sticky.is-talking .rtt-sticky__img, .rtt-sticky.is-talking .rtt-sticky__text { animation: none; } }
  @media print { .rtt-sticky { display: none; } }
  .rtt-sticky .rtt-sticky__ws { color: #ffd27a; font-weight: 800; text-decoration: underline; text-underline-offset: 2px; }
  .rtt-sticky .rtt-sticky__ws:hover { color: #fff; }
</style>

<script>
(function () {
  var RTT_STICKY = {
    title: 'Read my book, then explore real trains!',
    deal: '<span>Then scroll 650 train museums on <a class="rtt-sticky__ws" href="/whistle-stop#ride">Whistle&nbsp;Stop&nbsp;&rarr;</a></span>',
    titleMobile: 'Read my book!',             /* shorter lines for phones */
    dealMobile: '<span>Then explore <a class="rtt-sticky__ws" href="/whistle-stop#ride">Whistle&nbsp;Stop&nbsp;&rarr;</a></span>',
    button: 'Buy my book',
    stars: '5 stars on Amazon',
    url: 'https://www.amazon.com/dp/B0BLJG8VKL',
    cover: 'https://res.cloudinary.com/hxoymedx/image/upload/f_auto,q_auto,c_fill,ar_1:1,g_face,w_160/v1791475839/Screenshot_2026-10-08_at_9.10.00_AM.png',
    hideOn: ['/coloring-pages-download']        /* pages that never show the bar */
  };

  var path = location.pathname.replace(/\/+$/, '') || '/';
  if (RTT_STICKY.hideOn.indexOf(path) > -1) return;
  try { if (sessionStorage.getItem('rtt_sticky_off') === '1') return; } catch (e) {}
  if (document.body.classList.contains('sqs-edit-mode') || /\/config\//.test(location.href)) return;

  var mobile = window.matchMedia('(max-width: 859px)').matches;
  var bar = document.createElement('aside');
  bar.className = 'rtt-sticky'; bar.id = 'rtt-sticky'; bar.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Buy Rally Takes the Train');
  bar.innerHTML =
    '<img class="rtt-sticky__img" src="' + RTT_STICKY.cover + '" alt="Rally the corgi, tongue out, very excited" width="56" height="56" loading="lazy">' +
    '<div class="rtt-sticky__text"><span class="rtt-sticky__title">' + (mobile ? RTT_STICKY.titleMobile : RTT_STICKY.title) + '</span>' +
    '<span class="rtt-sticky__deal">' + (mobile ? RTT_STICKY.dealMobile : RTT_STICKY.deal) + '</span>' +
    '<span class="rtt-sticky__stars"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="currentColor" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M12 2.5l2.9 6 6.6.9-4.8 4.6 1.2 6.5L12 17.4 6.1 20.5l1.2-6.5L2.5 9.4l6.6-.9z"/></svg>' + RTT_STICKY.stars + '</span></div>' +
    '<a class="rtt-sticky__btn" href="' + RTT_STICKY.url + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">' +
    '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="currentColor" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="5.5" cy="10" r="2.2"/><circle cx="9.5" cy="5.5" r="2.4"/><circle cx="14.5" cy="5.5" r="2.4"/><circle cx="18.5" cy="10" r="2.2"/><path d="M12 10.5c-3.2 0-6 2.6-6 5.4 0 1.9 1.4 3.1 3.1 3.1 1 0 1.6-.4 2.9-.4s1.9.4 2.9.4c1.7 0 3.1-1.2 3.1-3.1 0-2.8-2.8-5.4-6-5.4z"/></svg>' +
    RTT_STICKY.button +
    '<span class="rtt-sticky__sr"> (opens in a new tab)</span></a>' +
    '<button class="rtt-sticky__x" type="button" aria-label="Dismiss the Buy my book bar">' +
    '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.4" stroke-linecap="round" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M6 6l12 12M18 6L6 18"/></svg></button>';
  document.body.appendChild(bar);

  function track(name, params) { try { if (typeof gtag === 'function') gtag('event', name, params || {}); } catch (e) {} }
  bar.addEventListener('click', function (e) { var w = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('.rtt-sticky__ws'); if (!w) return; track('sticky_cta_whistle_stop', { page_path: location.pathname }); if (/^\/whistle-stop/.test(location.pathname)) { e.preventDefault(); var b = document.querySelector('[data-ws-open]'); if (b) b.click(); } });

  /* threshold: the bottom of the first page section (the hero), else 70% of the viewport */
  function threshold() {
    var first = document.querySelector('.page-section');
    var cap = window.innerHeight * 1.2;                      /* never wait more than ~one screen */
    if (first) {
      var r = first.getBoundingClientRect(), bottom = r.bottom + window.scrollY - 40;
      if (bottom > 0) return Math.max(Math.min(bottom, cap), 240);
    }
    return window.innerHeight * 0.7;
  }
  /* don't double up with the on-page buy sections */
  function competitorVisible() {
    var els = document.querySelectorAll('.rtt-s6, .rtt-lp__final, .rtt-pf__buy, .rtt-dl__files, .rtt-ft');
    for (var i = 0; i < els.length; i++) {
      var r = els[i].getBoundingClientRect();
      if (r.top < window.innerHeight && r.bottom > 0) return true;
    }
    return false;
  }

  var shown = false, on = false, t = null, ticking = false;
  function padScroll() { try { var de = document.documentElement, hd = document.getElementById('header'); de.style.scrollPaddingBottom = on ? (bar.getBoundingClientRect().height + (window.innerWidth >= 860 ? 40 : 16)) + 'px' : ''; de.style.scrollPaddingTop = hd ? Math.round(hd.getBoundingClientRect().height + 12) + 'px' : ''; } catch (e) {} } /* keeps keyboard focus clear of the bar and the header (WCAG 2.4.11) */
  function update() {
    ticking = false;
    var want = window.scrollY > threshold() && !competitorVisible();
    if (want === on) return;
    on = want;
    bar.classList.toggle('is-on', on);
    document.body.classList.toggle('rtt-sticky-pad', on); padScroll();
    if (on && !shown) { shown = true; track('sticky_cta_shown', { page_path: location.pathname }); }
  }
  function onScroll() { if (!ticking) { ticking = true; requestAnimationFrame(update); } }
  window.addEventListener('scroll', onScroll, { passive: true });
  window.addEventListener('resize', onScroll);
  setTimeout(update, 400);

  /* ---- reactions to the Look Inside book (homepage). Keys come from data-key on the book pages. ---- */
  var REACT = {
    title:     ['Back to the start? Woof.',          'I never get tired of this book.',   'From the top!',          'Never gets old.'],
    morning:   ['Up early. Suitcase spotted.',        'Something big is happening.',       'Suitcase spotted.',      'Big day. Woof.'],
    station:   ['A TRAIN! A TRAIN!',                  'Dad had to boost me. No shame.',    'A TRAIN!',               'Dad boosted me.'],
    glitter:   ['Face paw. So much glitter.',         'I did not mean to litter.',         'So much glitter.',       'Face paw.'],
    window:    ['Ocean! Bird! Hello, bird!',          'Best seat on the Surfliner.',       'Hello, bird!',           'Best seat.'],
    pretzel:   ['Mom came back with a pretzel.',      'Woof. I love this page.',           'A pretzel!',             'I love this page.'],
    conductor: ['Mr. Conductor is my new friend.',    'I hoped the ride would never end.', 'New friend!',            'Never end, please.'],
    tunnel:    ['It got dark. I was brave.',          'Then the light came. Phew.',        'I was brave.',           'Then, light. Phew.'],
    arrival:   ['I see them! I see them!',            'Grandmommy and Papa are waiting.',  'I see them!',            'Grand-paw-rents!'],
    cta:       ['That&rsquo;s all the free pages. Woof.', 'The ending is waiting on Amazon.', 'Want the ending?',   'Get the book. Woof.']
  };
  /* what Rally says while the Look Inside book is on screen and nobody has turned a page yet */
  var INVITE = ['Boop! It&rsquo;s me, Rally. Turn the page.', 'Eight free pages. Go on, I&rsquo;ll wait.', 'Boop! Turn the page.', 'I&rsquo;ll wait. Woof.'];
  var titleEl = bar.querySelector('.rtt-sticky__title'), dealEl = bar.querySelector('.rtt-sticky__deal');
  var revertT = null, nextT = null, lastKey = null, inBook = false;
  function say(title, deal) {
    titleEl.innerHTML = title; dealEl.innerHTML = deal;
    bar.classList.remove('is-talking'); void bar.offsetWidth; bar.classList.add('is-talking');
  }
  function revert() {
    if (inBook) say(mobile ? INVITE[2] : INVITE[0], mobile ? INVITE[3] : INVITE[1]);
    else say(mobile ? RTT_STICKY.titleMobile : RTT_STICKY.title, mobile ? RTT_STICKY.dealMobile : RTT_STICKY.deal);
    lastKey = null;
  }
  /* invite while the book section is in view; normal copy everywhere else */
  var bookEl = document.getElementById('rtt-look-inside');
  if (bookEl && 'IntersectionObserver' in window) {
    new IntersectionObserver(function (entries) {
      var vis = entries[0].isIntersecting;
      if (vis === inBook) return;
      inBook = vis;
      clearTimeout(revertT); clearTimeout(nextT);
      if (inBook) { if (lastKey !== 'cta') revert(); }   /* keep the "want the ending?" line if they already reached it */
      else revert();
    }, { threshold: 0.3 }).observe(bookEl);
  }
  function react(key) {
    var lines = REACT[key];
    if (!lines || !document.body.contains(bar)) return;
    if (!on) { on = true; bar.classList.add('is-on'); document.body.classList.add('rtt-sticky-pad'); padScroll(); }  /* wake up if the book is in use */
    say(mobile ? lines[2] : lines[0], mobile ? lines[3] : lines[1]);
    if (key !== lastKey) track('sticky_cta_react', { page_key: key, page_path: location.pathname });
    lastKey = key;
    clearTimeout(revertT);
    if (key !== 'cta') revertT = setTimeout(revert, 9000);   /* back to the main line after a pause; the CTA line stays */
  }
  document.addEventListener('rtt:page', function (e) {
    var keys = (e.detail && e.detail.keys) || [e.detail && e.detail.key];
    clearTimeout(nextT);
    react(keys[0]);
    if (keys[1]) nextT = setTimeout(function () { react(keys[1]); }, 2600);   /* desktop spreads: one line per page */
  });

  bar.querySelector('.rtt-sticky__x').addEventListener('click', function () {
    on = false; bar.classList.remove('is-on'); document.body.classList.remove('rtt-sticky-pad'); padScroll();
    try { sessionStorage.setItem('rtt_sticky_off', '1'); } catch (e) {}
    window.removeEventListener('scroll', onScroll);
    track('sticky_cta_dismiss', { page_path: location.pathname });
    setTimeout(function () { bar.remove(); }, 300);
  });
})();
</script>

<!-- ============================================================
     ANALYTICS | Part 2 of 2: event layer
     Paste into: Settings → Advanced → Code Injection → FOOTER
     Sends GA4 events for every meaningful action on the site:
       buy_click            every click to Amazon (placement, link text, page type)
       cta_band_shown       the Look Inside buy band opened after the first page turn
       book_page_turn       Look Inside page turns (direction, page label, turn count)
       book_finished        reader reached the last page of the sample
       audience_card_click  Who It's For cards
       internal_link_click  links to landing pages, Our Story, classrooms, returns
       email_click          mailto links
       faq_open             FAQ accordion opened (question text)
       blog_category_click  category chips on the blog
       read_progress        25 / 50 / 75 / 100 % of a blog post or Our Story read
       returns_link_click   Amazon returns / Your Orders links on the returns page
       nav_click            header navigation clicks
       sticky_cta_shown     the sticky buy bar appeared (sent by sticky-cta.html); its Amazon clicks are buy_click with placement sticky_cta
       sticky_cta_dismiss   the sticky buy bar was closed
       lead_submit          coloring-pages Newsletter Block submitted; generate_lead fires on the download page
       file_download        PDF download clicks on the download page
       error_404            a 404 page was served (path)
     ============================================================ -->
<script>
(function () {
  if (typeof window.gtag !== 'function') return;

  var AMZ = /amazon\./i;
  function pageType() {
    var p = location.pathname.replace(/\/+$/, '') || '/';
    if (p === '/') return 'home';
    if (p === '/blog') return 'blog_index';
    if (p.indexOf('/blog/category/') === 0) return 'blog_category';
    if (p.indexOf('/blog/') === 0) return 'blog_post';
    if (p === '/our-story') return 'our_story';
    if (p === '/for-classrooms') return 'classrooms';
    if (p === '/coloring-pages') return 'coloring';
    if (p === '/coloring-pages-download') return 'coloring_download';
    if (p === '/free-book-for-teachers') return 'teachers';
    if (p === '/free-book-for-teachers-thank-you') return 'teachers_thank_you';
    if (['/corgi-childrens-book','/baby-shower-book-gift','/first-birthday-book-gift','/dog-book-for-kids','/train-books-for-toddlers','/books-about-visiting-grandparents'].indexOf(p) > -1) return 'landing';
    if (['/returns','/privacy','/terms-of-use','/accessibility'].indexOf(p) > -1) return 'legal';
    if (document.querySelector('.rtt-404')) return '404';
    return 'other';
  }
  var PT = pageType();
  function send(name, params) {
    params = params || {};
    params.page_type = PT;
    params.page_path = location.pathname;
    try { gtag('event', name, params); } catch (e) {}
  }
  function text(el) { return (el.textContent || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').replace(/\(opens in a new tab\)/, '').trim().slice(0, 80); }

  /* where on the page was the click? */
  var PLACEMENTS = [
    ['.rtt-sticky', 'sticky_cta'],
    ['.rtt-panel', 'hero'],
    ['.rtt-s1__cta', 'look_inside_band'],
    ['.rtt-pg--cta', 'look_inside_last_page'],
    ['.rtt-s1', 'look_inside'],
    ['.rtt-s2', 'our_story_section'],
    ['.rtt-s3', 'who_its_for'],
    ['.rtt-s4', 'reviews'],
    ['.rtt-s5', 'illustrator'],
    ['.rtt-s6', 'buy_section'],
    ['#get-pages, .newsletter-block', 'coloring_gate'],
    ['#request-form, .form-block', 'teacher_form'],
    ['.rtt-ty', 'teacher_thank_you'],
    ['.rtt-dl', 'download_page'],
    ['.rtt-lp__hero', 'lp_hero'],
    ['.rtt-lp__list', 'lp_list'],
    ['.rtt-lp__side', 'lp_sidebar'],
    ['.rtt-lp__final', 'lp_final'],
    ['.rtt-lp__prose', 'lp_body'],
    ['.rtt-post__cta', 'post_inline_cta'],
    ['.rtt-post', 'post_body'],
    ['.rtt-pf__buy', 'post_footer_buy'],
    ['.rtt-pf', 'post_footer'],
    ['.rtt-bh', 'blog_header'],
    ['.rtt-returns', 'returns_page'],
    ['.rtt-rp__side', 'returns_sidebar'],
    ['.rtt-404', 'page_404'],
    ['.rtt-lg', 'legal_page'],
    ['.rtt-ft', 'footer'],
    ['header, .header, #header', 'header']
  ];
  function placement(el) {
    for (var i = 0; i < PLACEMENTS.length; i++) { if (el.closest(PLACEMENTS[i][0])) return PLACEMENTS[i][1]; }
    return 'unknown';
  }

  /* ---------- clicks ---------- */
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var a = e.target.closest('a[href]'); if (!a) return;
    var href = a.getAttribute('href') || '';
    var where = placement(a);
    var label = text(a);

    if (AMZ.test(href)) {
      var isReturns = /gp\/help|order-history|customerReviews/.test(href);
      send(isReturns ? 'returns_link_click' : 'buy_click', { placement: where, link_text: label, link_url: href });
      return;
    }
    if (href.indexOf('mailto:') === 0) { send('email_click', { placement: where, link_text: label }); return; }
    if (a.hasAttribute('download') && !a.hasAttribute('data-rtt-file')) { send('file_download', { placement: where, link_text: label, link_url: href }); return; }
    if (a.closest('.rtt-s3__card')) { send('audience_card_click', { card: text(a.closest('.rtt-s3__card').querySelector('h3')), link_url: href }); return; }
    if (a.closest('.rtt-bh__cats')) { send('blog_category_click', { category: label }); return; }
    if (a.closest('header, .header, #header') && !AMZ.test(href)) { send('nav_click', { link_text: label, link_url: href }); return; }
    if (/^\/(our-story|for-classrooms|returns|corgi-childrens-book|baby-shower-book-gift|first-birthday-book-gift|dog-book-for-kids|train-books-for-toddlers|books-about-visiting-grandparents|coloring-pages|free-book-for-teachers|blog)/.test(href)) {
      send('internal_link_click', { placement: where, link_text: label, link_url: href });
    }
  }, true);

  /* ---------- FAQ accordions ---------- */
  document.addEventListener('toggle', function (e) {
    var d = e.target; if (!d || d.tagName !== 'DETAILS' || !d.open) return;
    var q = d.querySelector('summary'); send('faq_open', { question: q ? text(q) : '' });
  }, true);

  /* ---------- Look Inside book ---------- */
  var book = document.getElementById('rtt-look-inside');
  if (book && 'MutationObserver' in window) {
    var label = book.querySelector('[data-rtt-label]');
    var turns = 0, last = label ? label.textContent : '', bandSent = false, finishedSent = false;
    if (label) new MutationObserver(function () {
      var now = label.textContent.trim(); if (now === last) return;
      turns++;
      send('book_page_turn', { page_label: now, turn_count: turns, direction: /^Page 1\b/.test(now) ? 'back' : 'forward' });
      if (!finishedSent && /last stop|Page 4 of 4/i.test(now)) { finishedSent = true; send('book_finished', { turn_count: turns }); }
      last = now;
    }).observe(label, { childList: true, characterData: true, subtree: true });
    new MutationObserver(function () {
      if (!bandSent && book.classList.contains('is-turned')) { bandSent = true; send('cta_band_shown', {}); }
    }).observe(book, { attributes: true, attributeFilter: ['class'] });
  }

  /* ---------- reading depth on long pages ---------- */
  var article = document.querySelector('.rtt-post, .blog-item-content, .rtt-lp__prose');
  if (article && 'IntersectionObserver' in window && (PT === 'blog_post' || PT === 'our_story' || PT === 'landing' || PT === 'classrooms')) {
    var marks = [25, 50, 75, 100], sent = {};
    var sentinels = marks.map(function (m) {
      var s = document.createElement('span'); s.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
      s.style.cssText = 'position:absolute;left:0;width:1px;height:1px;top:' + m + '%;pointer-events:none';
      s.dataset.mark = m; return s;
    });
    if (getComputedStyle(article).position === 'static') article.style.position = 'relative';
    sentinels.forEach(function (s) { article.appendChild(s); });
    var io = new IntersectionObserver(function (entries) {
      entries.forEach(function (en) {
        var m = en.target.dataset.mark;
        if (en.isIntersecting && !sent[m]) { sent[m] = true; send('read_progress', { percent: +m, title: document.title.slice(0, 80) }); }
      });
    }, { threshold: 0 });
    sentinels.forEach(function (s) { io.observe(s); });
  }

  /* ---------- 404 ---------- */
  if (PT === '404') send('error_404', { missing_path: location.pathname, referrer: document.referrer.slice(0, 120) });
})();
</script>

<!-- ============================================================
     SHOP SCHEMA | Product, Breadcrumb and Shop ItemList JSON-LD
     Runs on /shop and /shop/p/*. Replaces Squarespace native Product block.
     ============================================================ -->
<script>
(function () {
  var path = location.pathname.replace(/\/+$/, '');
  var isProduct = path.indexOf('/shop/p/') === 0;
  var isShop = path === '/shop';
  if (!isProduct && !isShop) return;

  var ORIGIN = 'https://www.rallytakesthetrain.com';
  var ORG_ID = 'https://www.rallytakesthetrain.com/#org';
  var BOOK_ID = 'https://www.rallytakesthetrain.com/#book';

  function https(u) { return (u || '').replace(/^http:\/\//, 'https://'); }
  function text(html) {
    var d = document.createElement('div'); d.innerHTML = html || '';
    return (d.textContent || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
  }
  function add(obj) {
    var s = document.createElement('script');
    s.type = 'application/ld+json';
    s.setAttribute('data-rtt-schema', '1');
    s.text = JSON.stringify(obj);
    document.head.appendChild(s);
  }
  function removeNativeProduct() {
    var scripts = document.querySelectorAll('script[type="application/ld+json"]:not([data-rtt-schema])');
    for (var i = 0; i < scripts.length; i++) {
      try {
        var j = JSON.parse(scripts[i].text);
        if (j && j['@type'] === 'Product') scripts[i].parentNode.removeChild(scripts[i]);
      } catch (e) {}
    }
  }
  function priceValidUntil() {
    var d = new Date(); d.setFullYear(d.getFullYear() + 1);
    return d.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
  }
  function variantName(v) {
    var parts = [];
    for (var k in (v.attributes || {})) parts.push(v.attributes[k]);
    return parts.join(' / ');
  }

  fetch(location.pathname + '?format=json', { credentials: 'same-origin' })
    .then(function (r) { return r.json(); })
    .then(function (data) {
      if (isProduct && data.item) {
        var it = data.item;
        var url = ORIGIN + '/shop/p/' + it.urlId;
        var images = (it.items || []).map(function (im) { return https(im.assetUrl); }).filter(Boolean);
        if (!images.length && it.assetUrl) images = [https(it.assetUrl)];
        var desc = text(it.excerpt) || text(it.body);
        var variants = it.variants || [];
        var offers = variants.map(function (v) {
          var onSale = v.onSale && v.salePriceMoney && parseFloat(v.salePriceMoney.value) > 0;
          var price = onSale ? v.salePriceMoney.value : v.priceMoney.value;
          var inStock = v.unlimited || (v.qtyInStock > 0);
          var o = {
            '@type': 'Offer',
            'url': url,
            'price': price,
            'priceCurrency': v.priceMoney.currency || 'USD',
            'priceValidUntil': priceValidUntil(),
            'availability': inStock ? 'https://schema.org/InStock' : 'https://schema.org/OutOfStock',
            'itemCondition': 'https://schema.org/NewCondition',
            'seller': { '@id': ORG_ID }
          };
          if (v.sku) o.sku = v.sku;
          var vn = variantName(v);
          if (vn) o.name = it.title + ' (' + vn + ')';
          return o;
        });
        var prices = variants.map(function (v) { return parseFloat(v.priceMoney.value); }).filter(function (n) { return !isNaN(n); });
        var product = {
          '@context': 'https://schema.org',
          '@type': 'Product',
          '@id': url + '#product',
          'name': it.title,
          'description': desc,
          'image': images,
          'url': url,
          'sku': (variants[0] && variants[0].sku ? variants[0].sku.split('_')[0] : it.id),
          'brand': { '@type': 'Brand', 'name': 'Rally Takes the Train' },
          'manufacturer': { '@id': ORG_ID },
          'isRelatedTo': { '@id': BOOK_ID },
          'audience': { '@type': 'PeopleAudience', 'suggestedMinAge': 0 }
        };
        if (offers.length === 1) {
          product.offers = offers[0];
        } else if (offers.length > 1) {
          product.offers = {
            '@type': 'AggregateOffer',
            'url': url,
            'priceCurrency': 'USD',
            'lowPrice': Math.min.apply(null, prices).toFixed(2),
            'highPrice': Math.max.apply(null, prices).toFixed(2),
            'offerCount': offers.length,
            'availability': offers.some(function (o) { return o.availability.indexOf('InStock') > -1; }) ? 'https://schema.org/InStock' : 'https://schema.org/OutOfStock',
            'offers': offers
          };
        }
        removeNativeProduct();
        add(product);
        add({
          '@context': 'https://schema.org',
          '@type': 'BreadcrumbList',
          'itemListElement': [
            { '@type': 'ListItem', 'position': 1, 'name': 'Home', 'item': ORIGIN + '/' },
            { '@type': 'ListItem', 'position': 2, 'name': 'Shop', 'item': ORIGIN + '/shop' },
            { '@type': 'ListItem', 'position': 3, 'name': it.title, 'item': url }
          ]
        });
      }

      if (isShop && data.items) {
        add({
          '@context': 'https://schema.org',
          '@type': 'CollectionPage',
          '@id': ORIGIN + '/shop#page',
          'url': ORIGIN + '/shop',
          'name': 'Rally Takes the Train Shop',
          'isPartOf': { '@id': 'https://www.rallytakesthetrain.com/#website' },
          'about': { '@id': BOOK_ID },
          'mainEntity': {
            '@type': 'ItemList',
            'numberOfItems': data.items.length,
            'itemListElement': data.items.map(function (p, i) {
              var vs = p.variants || [];
              var ps = vs.map(function (v) { return parseFloat(v.priceMoney.value); });
              return {
                '@type': 'ListItem',
                'position': i + 1,
                'item': {
                  '@type': 'Product',
                  '@id': ORIGIN + '/shop/p/' + p.urlId + '#product',
                  'name': p.title,
                  'url': ORIGIN + '/shop/p/' + p.urlId,
                  'image': https(p.assetUrl),
                  'brand': { '@type': 'Brand', 'name': 'Rally Takes the Train' },
                  'offers': {
                    '@type': 'AggregateOffer',
                    'priceCurrency': 'USD',
                    'lowPrice': Math.min.apply(null, ps).toFixed(2),
                    'highPrice': Math.max.apply(null, ps).toFixed(2),
                    'offerCount': vs.length,
                    'availability': 'https://schema.org/InStock'
                  }
                }
              };
            })
          }
        });
      }
    })
    .catch(function () {});
})();
</script>

  var shown = false, prevFocus = null;
  var card = root.querySelector('.rta__card'), vv = window.visualViewport;
  // keep the sheet above the on-screen keyboard and inside the visible area
  function fit() {
    var h = vv ? vv.height : window.innerHeight;
    root.style.setProperty('--rta-vh', Math.round(h) + 'px');
    var kb = vv ? Math.max(0, Math.round(window.innerHeight - vv.height - vv.offsetTop)) : 0;
    root.style.paddingBottom = kb > 0 ? kb + 'px' : '';
  }
  function bind(on) {
    var f = on ? 'addEventListener' : 'removeEventListener';
    if (vv) { vv[f]('resize', fit); vv[f]('scroll', fit); } else window[f]('resize', fit);
  }
  var form = root.querySelector('form'), email = root.querySelector('#rta-email' + S + ''), city = root.querySelector('#rta-city' + S + ''), err = root.querySelector('#rta-err' + S + ''), go = root.querySelector('.rta__go'), sok = root.querySelector('#rta-sok' + S + '');
  function ga(n, p) { try { if (window.gtag) gtag('event', n, p || {}); } catch (e) {} }
  function fail(msg, field) { err.textContent = msg; err.hidden = false; if (field) { field.setAttribute('aria-invalid', 'true'); field.focus(); } }
  form.addEventListener('submit', function (e) {
    e.preventDefault(); err.hidden = true; email.removeAttribute('aria-invalid');
    var em = email.value.trim(), ct = city.value.trim();
    if (!/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]{2,}$/.test(em)) return fail('Please enter a valid email address.', email);
    if (root.querySelector('#rta-co' + S + '').value) { done(); return; }
    var q = {}; try { new URLSearchParams(location.search).forEach(function (v, k) { if (/^utm_/.test(k)) q[k] = v; }); } catch (x) {}
    var payload = { email: em, city: ct, state: stName, state_code: stCode, page_url: location.origin + path, page_title: document.title, last_search: lastTown, list: 'Christmas train ticket alerts 2027', source: 'Christmas train alert widget (' + PLACE + ' of page)', consent: 'One email when 2027 Christmas train tickets are released. Privacy policy: https://www.rallytakesthetrain.com/privacy', submitted_at: new Date().toISOString(), referrer: document.referrer || '', from_friend: invited ? 'yes' : 'no' };
    Object.keys(q).forEach(function (k) { payload[k] = q[k]; });
    go.disabled = true; go.lastChild.textContent = 'Adding you';
    var body = new URLSearchParams(payload);
    fetch(HOOK, { method: 'POST', mode: 'no-cors', body: body, keepalive: true }).then(done, function () {
      var ok = false; try { ok = navigator.sendBeacon && navigator.sendBeacon(HOOK, new URLSearchParams(payload)); } catch (x) {}
      if (ok) done(); else { go.disabled = false; go.lastChild.textContent = 'Alert me when tickets open'; fail('That did not go through. Check your connection and try again.'); }
    });
  });
  function say(t) { sok.textContent = ''; setTimeout(function () { sok.textContent = t; }, 60); }
  function copyLink(btn) {
    var ok = function () { btn.lastChild.textContent = 'Link copied'; say('Link copied. Paste it into a text or email to a friend.'); ga('train_alert_share', { method: 'copy' }); };
    var old = function () { var t = document.createElement('textarea'); t.value = shareUrl; t.setAttribute('readonly', ''); t.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;top:0;opacity:0'; root.appendChild(t); t.select(); var s = false; try { s = document.execCommand('copy'); } catch (y) {} root.removeChild(t); btn.focus(); if (s) ok(); else say('Copy this link to share: ' + shareUrl); };
    try { navigator.clipboard.writeText(shareUrl).then(ok, old); } catch (x) { old(); }
  }
  function doShare(btn) {
    if (navigator.share) navigator.share({ title: document.title, text: shareText, url: shareUrl }).then(function () { ga('train_alert_share', { method: 'native' }); say('Thanks for sharing.'); }, function (er) { if (!er || er.name !== 'AbortError') copyLink(btn); });
    else copyLink(btn);
  }
  // ---- share image for Instagram and TikTok stories and posts, drawn in the visitor's browser ----
  var BATP = 'M12 5.6c.3-.9.8-1.6 1.2-2l.3 1.6c.5-.1 1-.1 1.5 0 1.5-2 3.9-3.1 6.6-2.7-1 1-1.4 2.3-1.2 3.6-1.1.1-2 .8-2.5 1.8-.9-.4-1.9-.3-2.7.2-.5.7-.7 1.5-.6 2.4-.8-.7-1.7-1.1-2.6-1.1s-1.8.4-2.6 1.1c.1-.9-.1-1.7-.6-2.4-.8-.5-1.8-.6-2.7-.2-.5-1-1.4-1.7-2.5-1.8.2-1.3-.2-2.6-1.2-3.6 2.7-.4 5.1.7 6.6 2.7.5-.1 1-.1 1.5 0l.3-1.6c.4.4.9 1.1 1.2 2z';
  var imgApp = 'Instagram', imgFmt = 'story', imgFile = null, imgUrl = '', artP = null, renderN = 0, canFiles = false;
  try { canFiles = !!(navigator.canShare && navigator.canShare({ files: [new File(['x'], 'x.png', { type: 'image/png' })] })); } catch (x) {}
  var panel = root.querySelector('#rta-img' + S + ''), prev = root.querySelector('.rta__prev'), goImg = root.querySelector('[data-rta-go]'), tip = root.querySelector('.rta__tip');
  function art() { if (!artP) artP = new Promise(function (res) { var im = new Image(); im.crossOrigin = 'anonymous'; im.onload = function () { res(im); }; im.onerror = function () { res(null); }; im.src = TH.art; }); return artP; }
  function rr(c, x, y, w, h, r) { c.beginPath(); c.moveTo(x + r, y); c.arcTo(x + w, y, x + w, y + h, r); c.arcTo(x + w, y + h, x, y + h, r); c.arcTo(x, y + h, x, y, r); c.arcTo(x, y, x + w, y, r); c.closePath(); }
  function tx(c, t, x, y, font, color, ls) { c.font = font; c.fillStyle = color; if ('letterSpacing' in c) c.letterSpacing = (ls || 0) + 'px'; c.fillText(t, x, y); if ('letterSpacing' in c) c.letterSpacing = '0px'; }
  function makeImg(fmt) {
    var W = 1080, H = fmt === 'story' ? 1920 : 1350, s = fmt === 'story' ? 1 : .78;
    var fl = ['600 100px Fraunces', 'italic 500 100px Fraunces', '700 40px Nunito', '600 40px Nunito'].map(function (f) { try { return document.fonts.load(f).catch(function () {}); } catch (x) { return null; } });
    return Promise.all([art()].concat(fl)).then(function (r) {
      var im = r[0], cv = document.createElement('canvas'); cv.width = W; cv.height = H; var c = cv.getContext('2d');
      var g = c.createRadialGradient(W / 2, -100, 60, W / 2, H * .35, H); g.addColorStop(0, TH.bg[0]); g.addColorStop(.65, TH.bg[1]); g.addColorStop(1, TH.bg[2]); c.fillStyle = g; c.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
      var sd = 11; var rnd = function () { sd = (sd * 9301 + 49297) % 233280; return sd / 233280; };
      if (TH.deco === 'bats') {
        c.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,.75)'; for (var i = 0; i < 80; i++) { c.beginPath(); c.arc(rnd() * W, rnd() * H * .6, rnd() * 2.6 + .8, 0, 6.3); c.fill(); }
        var mx = W * .2, my = 210 * s, mr = 92 * s;
        c.save(); c.shadowColor = 'rgba(255,226,150,.5)'; c.shadowBlur = 80; var mg = c.createRadialGradient(mx - mr * .25, my - mr * .3, mr * .1, mx, my, mr); mg.addColorStop(0, '#fffbea'); mg.addColorStop(.55, '#f8e7ac'); mg.addColorStop(1, '#e9cd7c'); c.fillStyle = mg; c.beginPath(); c.arc(mx, my, mr, 0, 6.3); c.fill(); c.restore();
        var bat = new Path2D(BATP);
        [[mx - 6 * s, my - 44 * s, 4.2 * s, '#120b22'], [W * .6, 120 * s, 2.6 * s, '#7d6aa3'], [W * .78, 250 * s, 1.9 * s, '#7d6aa3']].forEach(function (b) { c.save(); c.translate(b[0], b[1]); c.scale(b[2], b[2]); c.fillStyle = b[3]; c.fill(bat); c.restore(); });
      } else {
        for (var j = 0; j < 140; j++) { var rad = rnd() * 4.5 + 1.2; c.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,' + (.35 + rnd() * .5).toFixed(2) + ')'; c.beginPath(); c.arc(rnd() * W, rnd() * H, rad, 0, 6.3); c.fill(); }
        var cols = ['#ee7b6c', '#f2c14e', '#5a9e74', '#7fb3d5', '#e05d7a'], segs = 4, sw = W / segs, top = 30 * s, sag = 70 * s, n = 0;
        c.strokeStyle = 'rgba(160,150,140,.7)'; c.lineWidth = 3;
        for (var q = 0; q < segs; q++) {
          var x0 = q * sw, x1 = x0 + sw; c.beginPath(); c.moveTo(x0, top); c.quadraticCurveTo(x0 + sw / 2, top + sag * 2, x1, top); c.stroke();
          for (var k = 1; k <= 4; k++) { var t = k / 5, bx = (1 - t) * (1 - t) * x0 + 2 * (1 - t) * t * (x0 + sw / 2) + t * t * x1, byy = (1 - t) * (1 - t) * top + 2 * (1 - t) * t * (top + sag * 2) + t * t * top;
            c.save(); c.translate(bx, byy); c.fillStyle = '#6b5d52'; c.fillRect(-6 * s, -2, 12 * s, 12 * s); c.shadowColor = cols[n % 5]; c.shadowBlur = 30; c.fillStyle = cols[n % 5]; c.beginPath(); c.ellipse(0, 28 * s, 13 * s, 20 * s, 0, 0, 6.3); c.fill(); c.restore(); n++; }
        }
      }
      c.textAlign = 'center'; var y = 430 * s;
      tx(c, ((stName ? stName + ' ' : '') + TH.season + ' train alert').toUpperCase(), W / 2, y, '700 ' + Math.round(34 * s) + 'px Nunito, sans-serif', TH.kicker, 4 * s);
      tx(c, TH.season + ' trains', W / 2, y + 125 * s, '600 ' + Math.round(108 * s) + 'px Fraunces, Georgia, serif', TH.head);
      tx(c, 'sell out fast', W / 2, y + 245 * s, 'italic 500 ' + Math.round(116 * s) + 'px Fraunces, Georgia, serif', TH.accent);
      var cy = y + 520 * s, R = 215 * s;
      c.save(); c.beginPath(); c.arc(W / 2, cy, R + 14 * s, 0, 6.3); c.fillStyle = TH.ring; c.fill(); c.beginPath(); c.arc(W / 2, cy, R, 0, 6.3); c.fillStyle = TH.artBg; c.fill(); c.clip();
      if (im) { var kk = Math.max(2 * R / im.width, 2 * R / im.height); c.drawImage(im, W / 2 - im.width * kk / 2, cy - im.height * kk / 2, im.width * kk, im.height * kk); }
      c.restore();
      var tw = 820 * s, th = 210 * s, tX = (W - tw) / 2, tY = cy + R + 70 * s;
      c.save(); c.translate(W / 2, tY + th / 2); c.rotate(-.025); c.translate(-W / 2, -(tY + th / 2));
      c.save(); c.shadowColor = 'rgba(0,0,0,.45)'; c.shadowBlur = 30; c.shadowOffsetY = 14; rr(c, tX, tY, tw, th, 26 * s); c.fillStyle = '#fbf5ea'; c.fill(); c.restore();
      c.textAlign = 'left';
      tx(c, 'ADMIT ONE FAMILY', tX + 50 * s, tY + 62 * s, '700 ' + Math.round(26 * s) + 'px Nunito, sans-serif', TH.tk, 5 * s);
      tx(c, TH.season + ' Train 2027', tX + 50 * s, tY + 125 * s, '600 ' + Math.round(54 * s) + 'px Fraunces, Georgia, serif', '#2b2622');
      tx(c, 'Boarding: ' + (stName || 'near you'), tX + 50 * s, tY + 175 * s, '600 ' + Math.round(32 * s) + 'px Nunito, sans-serif', '#5f5650');
      c.save(); c.translate(tX + tw - 140 * s, tY + th - 28 * s); c.rotate(-.12); c.lineWidth = 6 * s; c.strokeStyle = TH.stamp; c.fillStyle = 'rgba(251,245,234,.95)'; rr(c, -142 * s, -36 * s, 284 * s, 72 * s, 14 * s); c.fill(); c.stroke(); c.textAlign = 'center'; tx(c, 'ON THE LIST', 0, 12 * s, '700 ' + Math.round(31 * s) + 'px Nunito, sans-serif', TH.stamp, 3 * s); c.restore();
      c.restore();
      c.textAlign = 'center'; var by = tY + th + (fmt === 'story' ? 160 : 95) * s;
      tx(c, 'Get one email when 2027 tickets open', W / 2, by, '600 ' + Math.round(42 * s) + 'px Nunito, sans-serif', TH.sub);
      c.font = '700 ' + Math.round(44 * s) + 'px Nunito, sans-serif'; var pw = c.measureText('rallytakesthetrain.com').width + 96 * s;
      rr(c, (W - pw) / 2, by + 40 * s, pw, 96 * s, 48 * s); c.fillStyle = TH.ring; c.fill();
      tx(c, 'rallytakesthetrain.com', W / 2, by + 104 * s, '700 ' + Math.round(44 * s) + 'px Nunito, sans-serif', '#2b2622');
      return new Promise(function (res, rej) { try { cv.toBlob(function (b) { b ? res(b) : rej(); }, 'image/png'); } catch (x) { rej(x); } });
    });
  }
  function renderImg() {
    var n = ++renderN; imgFile = null; goImg.disabled = true; goImg.lastChild.textContent = 'Making your image';
    makeImg(imgFmt).then(function (b) {
      if (n !== renderN) return;
      imgFile = new File([b], 'rally-' + TH.season.toLowerCase() + '-train-alert-' + (stCode || 'us').toLowerCase() + '-' + imgFmt + '.png', { type: 'image/png' });
      if (imgUrl) URL.revokeObjectURL(imgUrl); imgUrl = URL.createObjectURL(b); prev.src = imgUrl;
      prev.alt = 'Preview of your ' + imgFmt + ' image: ' + TH.season + ' trains sell out fast, Rally the corgi, and a ' + (stName ? stName + ' ' : '') + '2027 ticket stamped On the list';
      goImg.disabled = false; goImg.lastChild.textContent = canFiles ? 'Share to ' + imgApp : 'Save image';
      tip.textContent = canFiles ? 'Pick ' + imgApp + ' in the share menu. We copy your link too, so you can paste it in a link sticker or your caption.' : 'Save the image, then post it to ' + imgApp + ' from your phone. We copy your link too, for a link sticker or your caption.';
      say('Your ' + imgApp + ' ' + imgFmt + ' image is ready.');
    }, function () { if (n === renderN) { goImg.lastChild.textContent = 'Image not available'; say('We could not make the image here. Use Share this page with a friend instead.'); } });
  }
  function openImg(btn) {
    imgApp = btn.getAttribute('data-rta-img') === 'tiktok' ? 'TikTok' : 'Instagram';
    [].forEach.call(root.querySelectorAll('[data-rta-img]'), function (b) { b.setAttribute('aria-expanded', b === btn ? 'true' : 'false'); });
    root.querySelector('[data-rta-app]').textContent = imgApp; panel.hidden = false; renderImg();
    var f = panel.querySelector('[aria-pressed="true"]'); if (f) f.focus({ preventScroll: true });
    setTimeout(function () { var rc = panel.getBoundingClientRect(), cc = card.getBoundingClientRect(); if (rc.bottom > cc.bottom) card.scrollTop += rc.bottom - cc.bottom + 16; }, 60);
  }
  function setFmt(f) { if (f === imgFmt) return; imgFmt = f; [].forEach.call(panel.querySelectorAll('[data-rta-fmt]'), function (b) { b.setAttribute('aria-pressed', b.getAttribute('data-rta-fmt') === f ? 'true' : 'false'); }); renderImg(); }
  function shareImg() {
    if (!imgFile) return;
    try { navigator.clipboard.writeText(shareUrl).catch(function () {}); } catch (x) {}
    var save = function () { var a = document.createElement('a'); a.href = imgUrl; a.download = imgFile.name; document.body.appendChild(a); a.click(); a.remove(); ga('train_alert_share', { method: imgApp.toLowerCase() + '_' + imgFmt + '_save' }); say('Image saved, and your link is copied.'); };
    if (canFiles) navigator.share({ files: [imgFile], title: document.title }).then(function () { ga('train_alert_share', { method: imgApp.toLowerCase() + '_' + imgFmt }); say('Shared. Your link is copied for a link sticker.'); }, function (er) { if (!er || er.name !== 'AbortError') save(); });
    else save();
  }
  function done() {
    root.classList.add('is-done'); subbed = true; [].forEach.call(document.querySelectorAll('.rta--inline'), function (r) { r.classList.add('is-done'); }); try { localStorage.setItem(KEY_SUB, String(Date.now())); } catch (e) {}
    ga('train_alert_signup', { state: stCode || 'none', placement: PLACE });
    art(); var h = root.querySelector('.rta__done h3'); if (h) { h.setAttribute('tabindex', '-1'); h.focus({ preventScroll: true }); }
  }
  email.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) { if (e.key === 'Enter' && !city.value && /@/.test(email.value)) { e.preventDefault(); city.focus(); } });
  root.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var ib = e.target.closest('[data-rta-img]'); if (ib) { openImg(ib); return; }
    var fb = e.target.closest('[data-rta-fmt]'); if (fb) { setFmt(fb.getAttribute('data-rta-fmt')); return; }
    if (e.target.closest('[data-rta-go]')) { shareImg(); return; }
    var sb = e.target.closest('[data-rta-share]'); if (sb) { doShare(sb); return; }
    var so = e.target.closest('a.rta__so'); if (so) ga('train_alert_share', { method: so.getAttribute('data-rta-m') });
    var a = e.target.closest('a.rta__g'); if (a) ga('train_alert_gift_click', { link_url: a.getAttribute('href') });
  });
  if (subbed) root.classList.add('is-done');
  return root;
  }
  // ---------- placement: top, middle and bottom of the page, plus a jump button under every ride ----------
  var reduce = !!(window.matchMedia && matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches);
  var KICK = { middle: 'Planning ahead for next year?', bottom: 'Before you go' + (stName ? ': the ' + esc(stName) + ' alert' : '') };
  function gaO(n, p) { try { if (window.gtag) gtag('event', n, p || {}); } catch (e) {} }
  function topBlock(el, box) { while (el && el.parentNode !== box) el = el.parentNode; return el; }
  var made = [];
  function watch(r, PLACE) {
    if (!('IntersectionObserver' in window)) return; var seen = false;
    var io = new IntersectionObserver(function (es) { es.forEach(function (e) { if (e.isIntersecting && !seen) { seen = true; gaO('train_alert_widget_view', { state: stCode || 'none', placement: PLACE, returning: subbed ? 'yes' : 'no' }); io.disconnect(); } }); }, { threshold: .35 });
    io.observe(r);
  }
  function put(PLACE, parent, before) { var r = make(PLACE === 'top' ? '' : '-' + PLACE, PLACE); parent.insertBefore(r, before || null); made.push(r); watch(r, PLACE); return r; }
  var BELL = '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><path d="M6 8a6 6 0 0 1 12 0c0 7 3 9 3 9H3s3-2 3-9"/><path d="M10.3 21a1.94 1.94 0 0 0 3.4 0"/></svg>';
  function jump(name, card, sold) {
    var b = document.createElement('button'); b.type = 'button'; b.className = 'rta-jump' + (card ? ' rta-jump--card' : ''); b.setAttribute('data-rta-jump', name || '');
    b.innerHTML = BELL + '<span><b>Get the 2027 ticket alert</b><small>' + (sold ? 'Sold out this year? Be first in line for 2027.' : 'One email the moment next year&rsquo;s tickets open.') + '</small></span>';
    if (name) b.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Get the 2027 ticket alert for ' + name);
    return b;
  }
  function nameOf(el) { var h = el && el.querySelector('h3, h2'); return h ? h.textContent.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').replace(/Sold out/i, '').trim() : ''; }
  function addButtons(scope) {
    [].forEach.call((scope || document).querySelectorAll('.rtt-pe-ride__book'), function (a) { var ride = a.closest('.rtt-pe-ride'); if (!ride || ride.querySelector('.rta-jump')) return; a.parentNode.insertBefore(jump(nameOf(ride), false, !!ride.querySelector('.rtt-pe-soldout')), a.nextSibling); });
    [].forEach.call((scope || document).querySelectorAll('.rtt-hx-book'), function (bk) { var sv = bk.nextElementSibling && bk.nextElementSibling.classList.contains('rtt-hx-save') ? bk.nextElementSibling : bk; if (sv.nextElementSibling && sv.nextElementSibling.classList.contains('rta-jump')) return; var t = (sv.getAttribute('data-title') || '').replace(/\s*\([^)]*\)\s*$/, ''); sv.parentNode.insertBefore(jump(t, false, false), sv.nextSibling); });
    [].forEach.call((scope || document).querySelectorAll('.xm-card__act, .hx-card__foot'), function (f) { var c = f.closest('.xm-card, .hx-card'); if (!c || c.querySelector('.rta-jump')) return; var h = c.querySelector('h3'); f.appendChild(jump(h ? h.textContent.trim() : '', true, c.getAttribute('data-sold') === '1')); });
  }
  function nearest(btn) {
    var y = btn.getBoundingClientRect().top + window.pageYOffset, best = made[0], bd = 1e12;
    made.forEach(function (r) { var rt = r.getBoundingClientRect(), t = rt.top + window.pageYOffset, b2 = t + rt.height, d = y < t ? t - y : y > b2 ? y - b2 : 0; if (d < bd) { bd = d; best = r; } });
    return best;
  }
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var btn = e.target.closest('[data-rta-jump]'); if (!btn || !made.length) return;
    var r = nearest(btn);
    r.scrollIntoView({ behavior: reduce ? 'auto' : 'smooth', block: 'center' });
    r.classList.add('is-flash'); setTimeout(function () { r.classList.remove('is-flash'); }, 1800);
    setTimeout(function () { var f = r.classList.contains('is-done') ? r.querySelector('[data-rta-share]') : r.querySelector('input[type="email"]'); if (f) f.focus({ preventScroll: true }); }, reduce ? 50 : 650);
    gaO('train_alert_jump', { ride: btn.getAttribute('data-rta-jump') || '', placement: r.getAttribute('data-place'), state: stCode || 'none' });
  });
  function mountAll() {
    if (made.length) return true;
    var post = [].filter.call(document.querySelectorAll('.rtt-post'), function (p) { return p.querySelector('.rtt-post__tldr'); })[0];
    if (post) {
      // the article is several .rtt-post blocks side by side; flatten them into one list of blocks
      var box = post.parentNode, blocks = [];
      [].forEach.call(box.children, function (c) { if (c.classList.contains('rtt-post')) [].forEach.call(c.children, function (k) { blocks.push(k); }); else blocks.push(c); });
      var tl = topBlock(post.querySelector('.rtt-post__tldr'), post), top = put('top', post, tl.nextSibling);
      var faq = box.querySelector('.rtt-post__faq'), fb = null;
      if (faq) { var fp = faq.closest('.rtt-post'); fb = topBlock(faq, fp); }
      var ti = blocks.indexOf(tl), bi = fb ? blocks.indexOf(fb) : blocks.length;
      var y0 = top.getBoundingClientRect().bottom, y1 = fb ? fb.getBoundingClientRect().top : box.getBoundingClientRect().bottom, mid = (y0 + y1) / 2, best = null, bd = 1e12;
      blocks.forEach(function (k, i) {
        if (i <= ti + 2 || i >= bi - 2 || !k.parentNode.classList.contains('rtt-post')) return;
        if (k.tagName !== 'H2' && !k.matches('.rtt-pe-ride, .rtt-rv') && !(k.tagName === 'SECTION' && k.firstElementChild && k.firstElementChild.tagName === 'H2')) return;
        var d = Math.abs(k.getBoundingClientRect().top - mid); if (d < bd) { bd = d; best = k; }
      });
      if (best) put('middle', best.parentNode, best);
      if (fb) put('bottom', fb.parentNode, fb); else put('bottom', post, null);
    } else {
      var lp = document.querySelector('article.rtt-lp'); if (!lp) return false;
      var res = lp.querySelector('section.xm-res, section.hx-res'); if (!res) return false;
      put('top', res.parentNode, res.nextSibling);
      var m = document.querySelector('section.rtt-near') || document.querySelector('section.rtt-s1'); if (m) put('middle', m.parentNode, m);
      var fq = document.querySelector('.rtt-lp__faq'); if (fq) put('bottom', fq.parentNode, fq);
    }
    addButtons();
    [].forEach.call(document.querySelectorAll('#xm-grid, #rtt-explore'), function (g) { if ('MutationObserver' in window) new MutationObserver(function () { addButtons(g); }).observe(g, { childList: true }); });
    if (invited || force) setTimeout(function () { made[0].classList.add('is-invited'); made[0].scrollIntoView({ behavior: reduce ? 'auto' : 'smooth', block: 'start' }); }, 700);
    return true;
  }
  if (!mountAll()) document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', mountAll);
})();
</script>

Settings > Advanced > Code Injection > Footer -->

  // keep the sheet above the on-screen keyboard and inside the visible area
  function fit() {
    var h = vv ? vv.height : window.innerHeight;
    root.style.setProperty('--rha-vh', Math.round(h) + 'px');
    var kb = vv ? Math.max(0, Math.round(window.innerHeight - vv.height - vv.offsetTop)) : 0;
    root.style.paddingBottom = kb > 0 ? kb + 'px' : '';
  }
  function bind(on) {
    var f = on ? 'addEventListener' : 'removeEventListener';
    if (vv) { vv[f]('resize', fit); vv[f]('scroll', fit); } else window[f]('resize', fit);
  }
  var form = root.querySelector('form'), email = root.querySelector('#rha-email' + S + ''), city = root.querySelector('#rha-city' + S + ''), err = root.querySelector('#rha-err' + S + ''), go = root.querySelector('.rha__go'), sok = root.querySelector('#rha-sok' + S + '');
  function ga(n, p) { try { if (window.gtag) gtag('event', n, p || {}); } catch (e) {} }
  function fail(msg, field) { err.textContent = msg; err.hidden = false; if (field) { field.setAttribute('aria-invalid', 'true'); field.focus(); } }
  form.addEventListener('submit', function (e) {
    e.preventDefault(); err.hidden = true; email.removeAttribute('aria-invalid');
    var em = email.value.trim(), ct = city.value.trim();
    if (!/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]{2,}$/.test(em)) return fail('Please enter a valid email address.', email);
    if (root.querySelector('#rha-co' + S + '').value) { done(); return; }
    var q = {}; try { new URLSearchParams(location.search).forEach(function (v, k) { if (/^utm_/.test(k)) q[k] = v; }); } catch (x) {}
    var payload = { email: em, city: ct, state: stName, state_code: stCode, page_url: location.origin + path, page_title: document.title, last_search: lastTown, list: 'Halloween train ticket alerts 2027', source: 'Halloween train alert widget (' + PLACE + ' of page)', consent: 'One email when 2027 Halloween train tickets are released.     Object.keys(q).forEach(function (k) { payload[k] = q[k]; });
    go.disabled = true; go.lastChild.textContent = 'Adding you';
    var body = new URLSearchParams(payload);
    fetch(HOOK, { method: 'POST', mode: 'no-cors', body: body, keepalive: true }).then(done, function () {
      var ok = false; try { ok = navigator.sendBeacon && navigator.sendBeacon(HOOK, new URLSearchParams(payload)); } catch (x) {}
      if (ok) done(); else { go.disabled = false; go.lastChild.textContent = 'Alert me when tickets open'; fail('That did not go through. Check your connection and try again.'); }
    });
  });
  function say(t) { sok.textContent = ''; setTimeout(function () { sok.textContent = t; }, 60); }
  function copyLink(btn) {
    var ok = function () { btn.lastChild.textContent = 'Link copied'; say('Link copied. Paste it into a text or email to a friend.'); ga('halloween_alert_share', { method: 'copy' }); };
    var old = function () { var t = document.createElement('textarea'); t.value = shareUrl; t.setAttribute('readonly', ''); t.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;top:0;opacity:0'; root.appendChild(t); t.select(); var s = false; try { s = document.execCommand('copy'); } catch (y) {} root.removeChild(t); btn.focus(); if (s) ok(); else say('Copy this link to share: ' + shareUrl); };
    try { navigator.clipboard.writeText(shareUrl).then(ok, old); } catch (x) { old(); }
  }
  function doShare(btn) {
    if (navigator.share) navigator.share({ title: document.title, text: shareText, url: shareUrl }).then(function () { ga('halloween_alert_share', { method: 'native' }); say('Thanks for sharing.'); }, function (er) { if (!er || er.name !== 'AbortError') copyLink(btn); });
    else copyLink(btn);
  }

  function rr(c, x, y, w, h, r) { c.beginPath(); c.moveTo(x + r, y); c.arcTo(x + w, y, x + w, y + h, r); c.arcTo(x + w, y + h, x, y + h, r); c.arcTo(x, y + h, x, y, r); c.arcTo(x, y, x + w, y, r); c.closePath(); }
  function tx(c, t, x, y, font, color, ls) { c.font = font; c.fillStyle = color; if ('letterSpacing' in c) c.letterSpacing = (ls || 0) + 'px'; c.fillText(t, x, y); if ('letterSpacing' in c) c.letterSpacing = '0px'; }
  function makeImg(fmt) {
    var W = 1080, H = fmt === 'story' ? 1920 : 1350, s = fmt === 'story' ? 1 : .78;
    var fl = ['600 100px Fraunces', 'italic 500 100px Fraunces', '700 40px Nunito', '600 40px Nunito'].map(function (f) { try { return document.fonts.load(f).catch(function () {}); } catch (x) { return null; } });
    return Promise.all([art()].concat(fl)).then(function (r) {
      var im = r[0], cv = document.createElement('canvas'); cv.width = W; cv.height = H; var c = cv.getContext('2d');
      var g = c.createRadialGradient(W / 2, -100, 60, W / 2, H * .35, H); g.addColorStop(0, TH.bg[0]); g.addColorStop(.65, TH.bg[1]); g.addColorStop(1, TH.bg[2]); c.fillStyle = g; c.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
      var sd = 11; var rnd = function () { sd = (sd * 9301 + 49297) % 233280; return sd / 233280; };
      if (TH.deco === 'bats') {
        c.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,.75)'; for (var i = 0; i < 80; i++) { c.beginPath(); c.arc(rnd() * W, rnd() * H * .6, rnd() * 2.6 + .8, 0, 6.3); c.fill(); }
        var mx = W * .2, my = 210 * s, mr = 92 * s;
        c.save(); c.shadowColor = 'rgba(255,226,150,.5)'; c.shadowBlur = 80; var mg = c.createRadialGradient(mx - mr * .25, my - mr * .3, mr * .1, mx, my, mr); mg.addColorStop(0, '#fffbea'); mg.addColorStop(.55, '#f8e7ac'); mg.addColorStop(1, '#e9cd7c'); c.fillStyle = mg; c.beginPath(); c.arc(mx, my, mr, 0, 6.3); c.fill(); c.restore();
        var bat = new Path2D(BATP);
        [[mx - 6 * s, my - 44 * s, 4.2 * s, '#120b22'], [W * .6, 120 * s, 2.6 * s, '#7d6aa3'], [W * .78, 250 * s, 1.9 * s, '#7d6aa3']].forEach(function (b) { c.save(); c.translate(b[0], b[1]); c.scale(b[2], b[2]); c.fillStyle = b[3]; c.fill(bat); c.restore(); });
      } else {
        for (var j = 0; j < 140; j++) { var rad = rnd() * 4.5 + 1.2; c.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,' + (.35 + rnd() * .5).toFixed(2) + ')'; c.beginPath(); c.arc(rnd() * W, rnd() * H, rad, 0, 6.3); c.fill(); }
        var cols = ['#ee7b6c', '#f2c14e', '#5a9e74', '#7fb3d5', '#e05d7a'], segs = 4, sw = W / segs, top = 30 * s, sag = 70 * s, n = 0;

            c.save(); c.translate(bx, byy); c.fillStyle = '#6b5d52'; c.fillRect(-6 * s, -2, 12 * s, 12 * s); c.shadowColor = cols[n % 5]; c.shadowBlur = 30; c.fillStyle = cols[n % 5]; c.beginPath(); c.ellipse(0, 28 * s, 13 * s, 20 * s, 0, 0, 6.3); c.fill(); c.restore(); n++; }

      c.restore();
      var tw = 820 * s, th = 210 * s, tX = (W - tw) / 2, tY = cy + R + 70 * s;

      c.save(); c.shadowColor = 'rgba(0,0,0,.45)'; c.shadowBlur = 30; c.shadowOffsetY = 14; rr(c, tX, tY, tw, th, 26 * s); c.fillStyle = '#fbf5ea'; c.fill(); c.restore();

      c.font = '700 ' + Math.round(44 * s) + 'px Nunito, sans-serif'; var pw = c.measureText('rallytakesthetrain.com').width + 96 * s;
      rr(c, (W - pw) / 2, by + 40 * s, pw, 96 * s, 48 * s); c.fillStyle = TH.ring; c.fill();
      tx(c, 'rallytakesthetrain.com', W / 2, by + 104 * s, '700 ' + Math.round(44 * s) + 'px Nunito, sans-serif', '#2b2622');
      return new Promise(function (res, rej) { try { cv.toBlob(function (b) { b ? res(b) : rej(); }, 'image/png'); } catch (x) { rej(x); } });
    });
  }
  function renderImg() {
    var n = ++renderN; imgFile = null; goImg.disabled = true; goImg.lastChild.textContent = 'Making your image';
    makeImg(imgFmt).then(function (b) {
      if (n !== renderN) return;
      imgFile = new File([b], 'rally-' + TH.season.toLowerCase() + '-train-alert-' + (stCode || 'us').toLowerCase() + '-' + imgFmt + '.png', { type: 'image/png' });
      if (imgUrl) URL.revokeObjectURL(imgUrl); imgUrl = URL.createObjectURL(b); prev.src = imgUrl;
      prev.alt = 'Preview of your ' + imgFmt + ' image: ' + TH.season + ' trains sell out fast, Rally the corgi, and a ' + (stName ? stName + ' ' : '') + '2027 ticket stamped On the list';
      goImg.disabled = false; goImg.lastChild.textContent = canFiles ? 'Share to ' + imgApp : 'Save image';
      tip.textContent = canFiles ? 'Pick ' + imgApp + ' in the share menu. We copy your link too, so you can paste it in a link sticker or your caption.' : 'Save the image, then post it to ' + imgApp + ' from your phone. We copy your link too, for a link sticker or your caption.';
      say('Your ' + imgApp + ' ' + imgFmt + ' image is ready.');
    }, function () { if (n === renderN) { goImg.lastChild.textContent = 'Image not available'; say('We could not make the image here. Use Share this page with a friend instead.'); } });
  }
  function openImg(btn) {
    imgApp = btn.getAttribute('data-rha-img') === 'tiktok' ? 'TikTok' : 'Instagram';
    [].forEach.call(root.querySelectorAll('[data-rha-img]'), function (b) { b.setAttribute('aria-expanded', b === btn ? 'true' : 'false'); });
    root.querySelector('[data-rha-app]').textContent = imgApp; panel.hidden = false; renderImg();
    var f = panel.querySelector('[aria-pressed="true"]'); if (f) f.focus({ preventScroll: true });
    setTimeout(function () { var rc = panel.getBoundingClientRect(), cc = card.getBoundingClientRect(); if (rc.bottom > cc.bottom) card.scrollTop += rc.bottom - cc.bottom + 16; }, 60);
  }
  function setFmt(f) { if (f === imgFmt) return; imgFmt = f; [].forEach.call(panel.querySelectorAll('[data-rha-fmt]'), function (b) { b.setAttribute('aria-pressed', b.getAttribute('data-rha-fmt') === f ? 'true' : 'false'); }); renderImg(); }
  function shareImg() {
    if (!imgFile) return;
    try { navigator.clipboard.writeText(shareUrl).catch(function () {}); } catch (x) {}
    var save = function () { var a = document.createElement('a'); a.href = imgUrl; a.download = imgFile.name; document.body.appendChild(a); a.click(); a.remove(); ga('halloween_alert_share', { method: imgApp.toLowerCase() + '_' + imgFmt + '_save' }); say('Image saved, and your link is copied.'); };
    if (canFiles) navigator.share({ files: [imgFile], title: document.title }).then(function () { ga('halloween_alert_share', { method: imgApp.toLowerCase() + '_' + imgFmt }); say('Shared. Your link is copied for a link sticker.'); }, function (er) { if (!er || er.name !== 'AbortError') save(); });
    else save();
  }
  function done() {
    root.classList.add('is-done'); subbed = true; [].forEach.call(document.querySelectorAll('.rha--inline'), function (r) { r.classList.add('is-done'); }); try { localStorage.setItem(KEY_SUB, String(Date.now())); } catch (e) {}
    ga('halloween_alert_signup', { state: stCode || 'none', placement: PLACE });
    art(); var h = root.querySelector('.rha__done h3'); if (h) { h.setAttribute('tabindex', '-1'); h.focus({ preventScroll: true }); }
  }
  email.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) { if (e.key === 'Enter' && !city.value && /@/.test(email.value)) { e.preventDefault(); city.focus(); } });
  root.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var ib = e.target.closest('[data-rha-img]'); if (ib) { openImg(ib); return; }
    var fb = e.target.closest('[data-rha-fmt]'); if (fb) { setFmt(fb.getAttribute('data-rha-fmt')); return; }
    if (e.target.closest('[data-rha-go]')) { shareImg(); return; }
    var sb = e.target.closest('[data-rha-share]'); if (sb) { doShare(sb); return; }
    var so = e.target.closest('a.rha__so'); if (so) ga('halloween_alert_share', { method: so.getAttribute('data-rha-m') });
    var a = e.target.closest('a.rha__g'); if (a) ga('halloween_alert_gift_click', { link_url: a.getAttribute('href') });
  });
  if (subbed) root.classList.add('is-done');
  return root;
  }
  // ---------- placement: top, middle and bottom of the page, plus a jump button under every ride ----------
  var reduce = !!(window.matchMedia && matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches);
  var KICK = { middle: 'Planning ahead for next year?', bottom: 'Before you go' + (stName ? ': the ' + esc(stName) + ' alert' : '') };
  function gaO(n, p) { try { if (window.gtag) gtag('event', n, p || {}); } catch (e) {} }
  function topBlock(el, box) { while (el && el.parentNode !== box) el = el.parentNode; return el; }
  var made = [];
  function watch(r, PLACE) {
    if (!('IntersectionObserver' in window)) return; var seen = false;
    var io = new IntersectionObserver(function (es) { es.forEach(function (e) { if (e.isIntersecting && !seen) { seen = true; gaO('halloween_alert_widget_view', { state: stCode || 'none', placement: PLACE, returning: subbed ? 'yes' : 'no' }); io.disconnect(); } }); }, { threshold: .35 });
    io.observe(r);
  }
  function put(PLACE, parent, before) { var r = make(PLACE === 'top' ? '' : '-' + PLACE, PLACE); parent.insertBefore(r, before || null); made.push(r); watch(r, PLACE); return r; }
  var BELL = '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><path d="M6 8a6 6 0 0 1 12 0c0 7 3 9 3 9H3s3-2 3-9"/><path d="M10.3 21a1.94 1.94 0 0 0 3.4 0"/></svg>';
  function jump(name, card, sold) {
    var b = document.createElement('button'); b.type = 'button'; b.className = 'rha-jump' + (card ? ' rha-jump--card' : ''); b.setAttribute('data-rha-jump', name || '');
    b.innerHTML = BELL + '<span><b>Get the 2027 ticket alert</b><small>' + (sold ? 'Sold out this year? Be first in line for 2027.' : 'One email the moment next year&rsquo;s tickets open.') + '</small></span>';
    if (name) b.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Get the 2027 ticket alert for ' + name);
    return b;
  }
  function nameOf(el) { var h = el && el.querySelector('h3, h2'); return h ? h.textContent.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').replace(/Sold out/i, '').trim() : ''; }
  function addButtons(scope) {
    [].forEach.call((scope || document).querySelectorAll('.rtt-pe-ride__book'), function (a) { var ride = a.closest('.rtt-pe-ride'); if (!ride || ride.querySelector('.rha-jump')) return; a.parentNode.insertBefore(jump(nameOf(ride), false, !!ride.querySelector('.rtt-pe-soldout')), a.nextSibling); });
    [].forEach.call((scope || document).querySelectorAll('.rtt-hx-book'), function (bk) { var sv = bk.nextElementSibling && bk.nextElementSibling.classList.contains('rtt-hx-save') ? bk.nextElementSibling : bk; if (sv.nextElementSibling && sv.nextElementSibling.classList.contains('rha-jump')) return; var t = (sv.getAttribute('data-title') || '').replace(/\s*\([^)]*\)\s*$/, ''); sv.parentNode.insertBefore(jump(t, false, false), sv.nextSibling); });
    [].forEach.call((scope || document).querySelectorAll('.xm-card__act, .hx-card__foot'), function (f) { var c = f.closest('.xm-card, .hx-card'); if (!c || c.querySelector('.rha-jump')) return; var h = c.querySelector('h3'); f.appendChild(jump(h ? h.textContent.trim() : '', true, c.getAttribute('data-sold') === '1')); });
  }
  function nearest(btn) {
    var y = btn.getBoundingClientRect().top + window.pageYOffset, best = made[0], bd = 1e12;
    made.forEach(function (r) { var rt = r.getBoundingClientRect(), t = rt.top + window.pageYOffset, b2 = t + rt.height, d = y < t ? t - y : y > b2 ? y - b2 : 0; if (d < bd) { bd = d; best = r; } });
    return best;
  }
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var btn = e.target.closest('[data-rha-jump]'); if (!btn || !made.length) return;
    var r = nearest(btn);
    r.scrollIntoView({ behavior: reduce ? 'auto' : 'smooth', block: 'center' });
    r.classList.add('is-flash'); setTimeout(function () { r.classList.remove('is-flash'); }, 1800);
    setTimeout(function () { var f = r.classList.contains('is-done') ? r.querySelector('[data-rha-share]') : r.querySelector('input[type="email"]'); if (f) f.focus({ preventScroll: true }); }, reduce ? 50 : 650);
    gaO('halloween_alert_jump', { ride: btn.getAttribute('data-rha-jump') || '', placement: r.getAttribute('data-place'), state: stCode || 'none' });
  });
  function mountAll() {
    if (made.length) return true;
    var post = [].filter.call(document.querySelectorAll('.rtt-post'), function (p) { return p.querySelector('.rtt-post__tldr'); })[0];
    if (post) {
      // the article is several .rtt-post blocks side by side; flatten them into one list of blocks
      var box = post.parentNode, blocks = [];
      [].forEach.call(box.children, function (c) { if (c.classList.contains('rtt-post')) [].forEach.call(c.children, function (k) { blocks.push(k); }); else blocks.push(c); });
      var tl = topBlock(post.querySelector('.rtt-post__tldr'), post), top = put('top', post, tl.nextSibling);
      var faq = box.querySelector('.rtt-post__faq'), fb = null;
      if (faq) { var fp = faq.closest('.rtt-post'); fb = topBlock(faq, fp); }
      var ti = blocks.indexOf(tl), bi = fb ? blocks.indexOf(fb) : blocks.length;
      var y0 = top.getBoundingClientRect().bottom, y1 = fb ? fb.getBoundingClientRect().top : box.getBoundingClientRect().bottom, mid = (y0 + y1) / 2, best = null, bd = 1e12;
      blocks.forEach(function (k, i) {
        if (i <= ti + 2 || i >= bi - 2 || !k.parentNode.classList.contains('rtt-post')) return;
        if (k.tagName !== 'H2' && !k.matches('.rtt-pe-ride, .rtt-rv') && !(k.tagName === 'SECTION' && k.firstElementChild && k.firstElementChild.tagName === 'H2')) return;
        var d = Math.abs(k.getBoundingClientRect().top - mid); if (d < bd) { bd = d; best = k; }
      });
      if (best) put('middle', best.parentNode, best);
      if (fb) put('bottom', fb.parentNode, fb); else put('bottom', post, null);
    } else {
      var lp = document.querySelector('article.rtt-lp'); if (!lp) return false;
      var res = lp.querySelector('section.xm-res, section.hx-res'); if (!res) return false;
      put('top', res.parentNode, res.nextSibling);
      var m = document.querySelector('section.rtt-near') || document.querySelector('section.rtt-s1'); if (m) put('middle', m.parentNode, m);
      var fq = document.querySelector('.rtt-lp__faq'); if (fq) put('bottom', fq.parentNode, fq);
    }
    addButtons();
    [].forEach.call(document.querySelectorAll('#xm-grid, #rtt-explore'), function (g) { if ('MutationObserver' in window) new MutationObserver(function () { addButtons(g); }).observe(g, { childList: true }); });
    if (invited || force) setTimeout(function () { made[0].classList.add('is-invited'); made[0].scrollIntoView({ behavior: reduce ? 'auto' : 'smooth', block: 'start' }); }, 700);
    return true;
  }
  if (!mountAll()) document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', mountAll);
})();
</script>
<!-- Rally outbound pop-up v2 (Nov 10, 2026): a boarding-pass pop-up on links to other websites names the destination, offers Continue and pitches the book. Continue is a real link: on phones, tablets and in-app browsers the site opens in a new tab and this tab stays on the guide (no Amazon redirect); on desktop the site opens in a new tab and this tab goes to the book on Amazon unless they clicked a book link. Skips Amazon, B&N, social, maps, add-to-calendar, mail/phone links, Rally's own pop-ups and modified clicks. Once per visit; never within 60 days of a book store click. Test: ?outboundtest. GA4: outbound_popup_view, _buy, _continue (mode = guide_stays | amazon_tab), _amazon_tab, _close, outbound_popup_result (continue | book_then_continue | book_only | stayed | closed | left_site). -->

  var TRAIN = '<svg class="rbo__train" viewBox="0 0 46 22" aria-hidden="true"><rect x="1" y="5" width="26" height="12" rx="3" fill="#ee7b6c"/><rect x="4" y="8" width="6" height="5" rx="1" fill="#172235"/><rect x="13" y="8" width="6" height="5" rx="1" fill="#172235"/><rect x="29" y="2" width="14" height="15" rx="3" fill="#fbf5ea"/><rect x="32" y="5" width="8" height="5" rx="1" fill="#172235"/><circle cx="8" cy="19" r="2.4" fill="#fbf5ea"/><circle cx="20" cy="19" r="2.4" fill="#fbf5ea"/><circle cx="36" cy="19" r="2.4" fill="#ee7b6c"/></svg>';

          '<p class="rbo__ride" id="rbo-ride" data-rbo-ride></p>' +
          '<div class="rbo__line" aria-hidden="true"><b>Rally&rsquo;s guide</b><span class="rbo__track">' + TRAIN + '</span><b data-rbo-host2></b></div>' +

        '</div>' +
        '<div class="rbo__perf" aria-hidden="true"></div>' +
