          '<div><p class="rbo__sk">' + COPY.k + '</p><h3 class="rbo__bh">' + COPY.h + '</h3><p class="rbo__bp">' + COPY.p + '</p>' +
            '<p class="rbo__rate"><span class="rbo__st">' + STAR + STAR + STAR + STAR + STAR + '</span><b>5.0</b><span class="rbo__rc">29 ratings on Amazon</span><span class="rbo__sr">Rated 5.0 out of 5 stars from 29 ratings on Amazon</span></p></div>' +
          '<div class="rbo__cta" style="grid-column:1 / -1">' +
            '<a class="rbo__buy" data-rbo-m="amazon_paperback" href="' + AMZ + '"' + BLANK + '><span class="rbo__lbl"><b>Get the book</b><small>Paperback on Amazon' + NEWTAB + '</small></span><span class="rbo__chip" aria-hidden="true">' + ARROW + '</span></a>' +
            '<p class="rbo__alts">Also on <a class="rbo__alt" data-rbo-m="amazon_kindle" href="' + KINDLE + '"' + BLANK + '>Kindle' + NEWTAB + '</a> and <a class="rbo__alt" data-rbo-m="barnes_noble" href="' + BN + '"' + BLANK + '>Barnes &amp; Noble' + NEWTAB + '</a></p>' +
            '<p class="rbo__free">' + FREE + '</p>' +
          '</div>' +
        '</div>' +
        '<div class="rbo__foot"><button type="button" class="rbo__stay" data-rbo-close>Stay on Rally&rsquo;s guide</button></div>' +
      '</div>' +
    '</div>';

  var card = root.querySelector('.rbo__card'), scroller = root.querySelector('.rbo__scroll'), goBtn = root.querySelector('[data-rbo-go]');
  var mq = window.matchMedia ? matchMedia('(max-width: 640px)') : { matches: false }, vv = window.visualViewport;
  var cur = null, prevFocus = null, inerted = [], isOpen = false, openedAt = 0, chosen = '', resultSent = false;
  // phones, tablets, in-app browsers: Continue is a plain link and this tab stays on the guide; only desktop swaps this tab to Amazon
  var IN_APP = /FBAN|FBAV|FB_IAB|Instagram|TikTok|musical_ly|BytedanceWebview|Snapchat|Pinterest|Line\/|GSA\/|; wv\)/i, HANDHELD = /Android|iPhone|iPad|iPod|Mobile|Silk/i;
  function stays() {
    var ua = navigator.userAgent || '';
    if (mq.matches || HANDHELD.test(ua) || IN_APP.test(ua)) return true;
    if (/Macintosh/.test(ua) && navigator.maxTouchPoints > 1) return true; // iPad
    try { return matchMedia('(hover: none) and (pointer: coarse)').matches; } catch (e) { return false; }
  }
  // one result per pop-up, so GA shows exactly what each visitor chose:
  // continue (went to the ride site only), book_then_continue, book_only, stayed (Stay on Rally's guide), closed (X, Escape, backdrop, swipe), left_site
  function result(r) { if (resultSent || !cur) return; resultSent = true; ga('outbound_popup_result', { result: r, bought_book: chosen ? 'yes' : 'no', store: chosen || 'none', link_domain: cur.host, ride: cur.ride, page_type: page, seconds_open: Math.round((Date.now() - openedAt) / 1000), transport_type: 'beacon' }); }

  function ga(n, p) { try { if (window.gtag) gtag('event', n, p || {}); } catch (e) {} }
  function esc(s) { return String(s).replace(/[&<>"]/g, function (c) { return { '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;' }[c]; }); }
  function clean(s) { return (s || '').replace(/\(opens in a new tab\)/gi, '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').replace(/[↗→]/g, '').trim(); }
  function store(k) { try { return localStorage.getItem(k); } catch (e) { return null; } }
  function sess(k, v) { try { if (v === undefined) return sessionStorage.getItem(k); sessionStorage.setItem(k, v); } catch (e) { return null; } }
  function fit() { root.style.setProperty('--rbo-vh', Math.round(vv ? vv.height : window.innerHeight) + 'px'); }
  // name the ride the link belongs to: the card or section heading, else the closest heading above it
  function rideName(a) {
    var box = a.closest('.rtt-pe-ride, .rtt-rv, .hx-card, .xm-card, .rtt-hx-ride');
    var h = box && box.querySelector('h2, h3, h4');
    if (!h) {
      var hs = [].slice.call(document.querySelectorAll('h2, h3')).filter(function (x) { return x.compareDocumentPosition(a) & Node.DOCUMENT_POSITION_FOLLOWING; });
      h = hs[hs.length - 1];
      if (h && a.getBoundingClientRect().top - h.getBoundingClientRect().top > 2400) h = null;
    }
    if (!h) return '';
    var c = h.cloneNode(true); [].forEach.call(c.querySelectorAll('.rtt-pe-soldout, .rtt-sr, [aria-hidden="true"]'), function (n) { n.remove(); });
    return clean(c.textContent).replace(/[:–—-]\s*$/, '').slice(0, 140);
  }
  function rideMeta(a) {
    var box = a.closest('.hx-card, .xm-card'), m = box && box.querySelector('.hx-card__m, .xm-card__where');
    if (!m) return '';
    var c = m.cloneNode(true); [].forEach.call(c.querySelectorAll('.rtt-sr, [aria-hidden="true"], svg'), function (n) { n.remove(); });
    return clean(c.textContent).slice(0, 120);
  }
  function linkLabel(a) {
    var c = a.cloneNode(true); [].forEach.call(c.querySelectorAll('.rtt-sr, .rbo__sr, [aria-hidden="true"], svg'), function (n) { n.remove(); });
    [].forEach.call(c.querySelectorAll('small, br'), function (n) { n.parentNode.insertBefore(document.createTextNode(' '), n); });
    return clean(c.textContent).slice(0, 120);
  }

  function outbound(a, e) {
    if (!a || !a.href || a.hasAttribute('download') || a.hasAttribute('data-no-rbo')) return null;
    if (e.defaultPrevented || e.button !== 0 || e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey || e.shiftKey || e.altKey) return null;
    if (a.closest('.rbo, .rbs, .rta, .rha, .rtt-cal, [data-no-rbo]')) return null;
    var u; try { u = new URL(a.href, location.href); } catch (x) { return null; }
    if (!/^https?:$/.test(u.protocol) || u.host === location.host) return null;
    var host = u.hostname.replace(/^www\./, '');
    if (SKIP_HOST.test(host) || SKIP_URL.test(u.href)) return null;
    return { url: u.href, host: host };
  }

  // shrink a long website name until its longest piece fits on one line
  function fitHost() {
    var h = root.querySelector('.rbo__h'), el = root.querySelector('[data-rbo-host]'); el.style.fontSize = '';
    var size = parseFloat(getComputedStyle(el).fontSize);
    while (h.scrollWidth > h.clientWidth + 1 && size > 17) { size -= 1; el.style.fontSize = size + 'px'; }
    el.style.overflowWrap = h.scrollWidth > h.clientWidth + 1 ? 'anywhere' : '';
  }
  function open(a, d) {
    var label = linkLabel(a), ride = rideName(a), meta = rideMeta(a);
    var tickets = TICKET_HOST.test(d.host) || /ticket|book|reserv|dates/i.test(label);
    if (/^tickets?( on the railroad.s site)?$/i.test(label)) label = '';
    cur = { url: d.url, host: d.host, ride: ride, a: a };
    root.querySelector('[data-rbo-kick]').textContent = tickets ? 'Boarding pass · tickets' : 'Boarding pass';
    root.querySelector('[data-rbo-host]').innerHTML = esc(d.host).replace(/\./g, '.<wbr>');
    root.classList.toggle('is-long', d.host.length > 20);
    root.querySelector('[data-rbo-host2]').textContent = d.host;
    root.querySelector('[data-rbo-golabel]').textContent = tickets ? 'Continue to tickets' : 'Continue to the website';
    cur.stay = stays(); cur.tickets = tickets; goBtn.href = d.url;
    root.classList.toggle('is-stay', cur.stay);
    root.querySelector('[data-rbo-gosub]').textContent = cur.stay ? 'Opens in a new tab. Rally\u2019s guide stays open here.' : 'Opens in a new tab, and Rally\u2019s book opens here on Amazon';
    var rideEl = root.querySelector('[data-rbo-ride]');
    var sub = meta || (label && label.toLowerCase() !== ride.toLowerCase() ? label : '');
    if (ride && sub) rideEl.innerHTML = '<b>' + esc(ride) + '</b><br>' + esc(sub);
    else if (ride) rideEl.innerHTML = (tickets ? 'Tickets for ' : '') + '<b>' + esc(ride) + '</b>';
    else rideEl.innerHTML = label ? esc(label) : 'A website outside Rally&rsquo;s guide.';
    var fav = root.querySelector('[data-rbo-fav]'), img = new Image();
    img.alt = ''; img.width = 32; img.height = 32;
    img.onload = function () { if (img.naturalWidth > 16) { fav.innerHTML = ''; fav.appendChild(img); } };
    fav.innerHTML = TICKET;
    img.src = 'https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?sz=64&domain=' + encodeURIComponent(d.host);
    var cov = root.querySelector('[data-rbo-src]'); if (cov) { cov.src = cov.getAttribute('data-rbo-src'); cov.removeAttribute('data-rbo-src'); }
    root.classList.remove('is-thanked'); root.querySelector('[data-rbo-thx]').textContent = '';

    isOpen = true; sess(KEY_SHOWN, '1'); openedAt = Date.now(); chosen = ''; resultSent = false;
    document.body.appendChild(root); root.hidden = false; prevFocus = a;
    document.documentElement.classList.add('rbo-lock'); fit(); if (vv) vv.addEventListener('resize', fit); else window.addEventListener('resize', fit);
    [].forEach.call(document.body.children, function (n) { if (n !== root && !n.inert && n.tagName !== 'SCRIPT') { n.inert = true; inerted.push(n); } });
    scroller.scrollTop = 0;
    requestAnimationFrame(function () { requestAnimationFrame(function () { fitHost(); root.classList.add('is-open'); card.focus({ preventScroll: true }); }); });
    document.addEventListener('keydown', onKey);
    ga('outbound_popup_view', { link_domain: d.host, link_url: d.url, ride: ride || label, page_type: page, layout: mq.matches ? 'sheet' : 'dialog' });
  }
  function close(how, keepFocus) {
    if (!isOpen) return; isOpen = false;
    root.classList.remove('is-open'); document.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey);
    document.documentElement.classList.remove('rbo-lock'); if (vv) vv.removeEventListener('resize', fit); else window.removeEventListener('resize', fit);
    inerted.forEach(function (n) { n.inert = false; }); inerted = [];
    setTimeout(function () { root.hidden = true; card.style.transform = ''; root.classList.remove('is-drag'); }, mq.matches ? 430 : 280);
    if (!keepFocus && prevFocus && prevFocus.focus) try { prevFocus.focus({ preventScroll: true }); } catch (e) {}
    if (how) { ga('outbound_popup_close', { method: how, link_domain: cur && cur.host, page_type: page }); result(chosen ? 'book_only' : how === 'stay' ? 'stayed' : 'closed'); }
  }
  function go(e) {
    if (!cur) return;
    var stay = cur.stay || !!chosen || e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey || e.shiftKey || e.altKey;
    ga('outbound_popup_continue', { link_domain: cur.host, link_url: cur.url, ride: cur.ride, page_type: page, after_book: chosen ? 'yes' : 'no', mode: stay ? 'guide_stays' : 'amazon_tab', transport_type: 'beacon' });
    result(chosen ? 'book_then_continue' : 'continue');
    if (stay) {
      var from = prevFocus; setTimeout(function () { close('', true); if (from && from.focus) try { from.focus({ preventScroll: true }); } catch (x) {} }, 80);
      return;
    }
    e.preventDefault();
    var w = null; try { w = window.open(cur.url, '_blank'); } catch (e) {}
    if (!w) { location.href = cur.url; return; } // pop-up blocked: go in this tab
    try { w.opener = null; } catch (e) {}
    // browsers allow one new tab per click, so the ride site opens in a new tab and this tab goes to the book on Amazon
    ga('outbound_popup_amazon_tab', { link_domain: cur.host, ride: cur.ride, page_type: page, transport_type: 'beacon' });
    isOpen = false; setTimeout(function () { location.href = AMZ; }, 150);
  }
  function onKey(e) {
    if (e.key === 'Escape') { close('escape'); return; }
    if (e.key !== 'Tab') return;
    var f = [].filter.call(root.querySelectorAll('a[href], button'), function (n) { return n.offsetParent !== null && getComputedStyle(n).visibility !== "hidden"; }); if (!f.length) return;
    var first = f[0], last = f[f.length - 1];
    if (e.shiftKey && document.activeElement === first) { e.preventDefault(); last.focus(); }
    else if (!e.shiftKey && document.activeElement === last) { e.preventDefault(); first.focus(); }
  }
  root.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    if (e.target.closest('[data-rbo-close]')) { close(e.target.closest('.rbo__scrim') ? 'scrim' : e.target.closest('.rbo__stay') ? 'stay' : 'button'); return; }
    if (e.target.closest('[data-rbo-go]')) { go(e); return; }
    var a = e.target.closest('a[data-rbo-m]');
    if (a) {
      var m = a.getAttribute('data-rbo-m');
      ga('outbound_popup_buy', { store: m, link_domain: cur && cur.host, page_type: page }); chosen = chosen || m;
      try { localStorage.setItem(KEY_BUY, String(Date.now())); } catch (x) {}
      root.classList.add('is-thanked');
      root.querySelector('[data-rbo-thx]').textContent = 'Thank you for supporting Rally’s guide! Your ride is still waiting:';
      root.querySelector('[data-rbo-golabel]').textContent = cur && cur.tickets ? 'Now continue to your tickets' : 'Now continue to your ride';
      root.querySelector('[data-rbo-gosub]').textContent = 'Opens in a new tab';
      setTimeout(function () { scroller.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' }); goBtn.focus({ preventScroll: true }); }, 400);
    }
  });
  // phones: pull the sheet down (from the top) to close
  var sy = null, dy = 0;
  card.addEventListener('touchstart', function (e) { if (!mq.matches || scroller.scrollTop > 0 || e.target.closest('a, button')) { sy = null; return; } sy = e.touches[0].clientY; dy = 0; }, { passive: true });
  card.addEventListener('touchmove', function (e) { if (sy === null) return; dy = e.touches[0].clientY - sy; if (dy > 0) { root.classList.add('is-drag'); card.style.transform = 'translateY(' + dy + 'px)'; } }, { passive: true });
  card.addEventListener('touchend', function () { if (sy === null) return; root.classList.remove('is-drag'); sy = null; if (dy > 110) close('swipe'); else card.style.transform = ''; });

  function leaving() { if (isOpen) result(chosen ? 'book_only' : 'left_site'); }
  window.addEventListener('beforeunload', leaving); window.addEventListener('pagehide', leaving);
  window.addEventListener('pageshow', function (e) { if (e.persisted && isOpen && resultSent) close('', true); });
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    if (isOpen) return;
    var a = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('a[href]'); if (!a) return;
    var d = outbound(a, e); if (!d) return;
    if (!force) {
      if (sess(KEY_SHOWN)) return; // once per visit
      if (Date.now() - (+store(KEY_BUY) || 0) < 60 * DAY) return; // already bought the book
    }
    // never stack on another open pop-up
    if (document.querySelector('.rbs.is-open') || [].some.call(document.querySelectorAll('[aria-modal="true"]'), function (n) { return !root.contains(n) && n.offsetParent !== null; })) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    open(a, d);
  });
})();
</script>

This keeps one WebSite and one Article per page for crawlers that render the page. Product markup is left alone. -->
<script id="rtt-schema-clean">
(function () {

parsed.forEach(function (d) {
if (!d) return;
(d["@graph"] || [d]).forEach(function (n) {
if (!n || !n["@id"]) return;
if (is(n, "WebSite")) hasSite = true;
if (is(n, "Article") || is(n, "BlogPosting") || is(n, "NewsArticle") || is(n, "LiveBlogPosting")) hasArticle = true;
});
});

});

region]
  var LN = {en: 'English', fr: 'French', de: 'German', es: 'Spanish', nl: 'Dutch', it: 'Italian', pt: 'Portuguese', ja: 'Japanese', ar: 'Arabic', am: 'Amharic', mg: 'Malagasy', hr: 'Croatian', el: 'Greek', da: 'Danish', et: 'Estonian', fi: 'Finnish', uk: 'Ukrainian', pl: 'Polish', ko: 'Korean', ro: 'Romanian', sv: 'Swedish', hu: 'Hungarian', nb: 'Norwegian', sk: 'Slovak', lt: 'Lithuanian', lv: 'Latvian', sl: 'Slovenian', is: 'Icelandic'};
  var norm = function (s) { return String(s || '').toLowerCase().normalize('NFD').replace(/[̀-ͯ]/g, '').replace(/[^a-z0-9぀-ヿ一-鿿]+/g, ' ').trim(); };
  var path = location.pathname.replace(/\/+$/, '') || '/';
  var slug = /^\/christmas-events\//.test(path) ? path.split('/').pop() : '';
  var me = P[slug] || null;
  var sec = me ? me[1] : (/^\/christmas-events(-for-kids)?$/.test(path) ? 'us' : '');
  function list(s) { var out = []; Object.keys(P).forEach(function (k) { var v = P[k]; if (v[1] === (s || sec || 'us') && v[2] === 'c' && out.indexOf(v[0]) < 0) out.push(v[0]); }); return out.sort(function (a, b) { return a.localeCompare(b); }); }
  function match(city) {
    var n = norm(city); if (!n) return null; var best = null;
    Object.keys(P).forEach(function (k) { var v = P[k]; if (v[2] === 'h') return; var m = norm(v[0]); if (m === n || (n.length > 3 && (m.indexOf(n) === 0 || n.indexOf(m) === 0))) { var score = (v[1] === sec ? 2 : 0) + (m === n ? 1 : 0); if (!best || score > best.s) best = {s: score, k: k, v: v}; } });
    return best ? {name: best.v[0], slug: best.k, url: location.origin + '/christmas-events/' + best.k, section: best.v[1], region: best.v[3] || ''} : null;
  }
  function pageCity() { return me && me[2] === 'c' ? me[0] : ''; }
  function example() {
    if (me && me[2] === 's') { var r = me[3]; var hit = Object.keys(P).filter(function (k) { return P[k][1] === 'us' && P[k][2] === 'c' && P[k][3] === r; })[0]; if (hit) return P[hit][0]; }
    var s0 = sec || 'us', k0 = Object.keys(P).filter(function (k) { return P[k][1] === s0 && P[k][2] === 'c'; })[0]; return k0 ? P[k0][0] : '';
  }
  // the city they typed before, or the town they searched in the events finder
  function saved() { try { return localStorage.getItem('ke-city') || (localStorage.getItem('xm-last') || '').replace(/,\s*[A-Z]{2}$/, ''); } catch (e) { return ''; } }
  function remember(c) { try { if (c) localStorage.setItem('ke-city', c); } catch (e) {} }
  function payload(o) {
    var b = new URLSearchParams(), q = new URLSearchParams(location.search), L = o.lang || 'en', C = o.country || 'US';
    var city = String(o.city || '').trim(), pc = pageCity(), m = match(city);
    var put = function (k, v) { b.set(k, v == null ? '' : String(v)); };
    put('email', o.email);
    put('city', city); put('city_edited', city && pc && norm(city) !== norm(pc) ? 'yes' : (city && !pc ? 'typed' : 'no'));
    put('city_in_guide', m ? 'yes' : 'no'); put('city_guide_name', m ? m.name : ''); put('city_guide_url', m ? m.url : ''); put('city_region', m ? m.region : '');
    put('page_city', pc); put('page_type', me ? ({c: 'city', s: 'state', h: 'country'}[me[2]]) : (/^\/christmas-events(-for-kids)?$/.test(path) ? 'hub' : 'other')); put('page_region', me ? (me[3] || '') : (o.state || ''));
    put('lang', L); put('language', LN[L] || L); put('locale', L + '-' + C); put('country', C); put('section', sec || (C === 'US' ? 'us' : ''));
    put('state', o.state || ''); put('page', path); put('page_url', location.href.split('#')[0]); put('page_title', document.title);
    put('source', o.source); put('list', o.list); put('subscribe', 'rally-blog'); put('event_link', o.event || ''); put('from_friend', o.friend ? 'yes' : 'no');
    put('referrer', document.referrer || ''); put('submitted_at', new Date().toISOString()); put('timezone', (function () { try { return Intl.DateTimeFormat().resolvedOptions().timeZone || ''; } catch (e) { return ''; } })()); put('browser_language', navigator.language || '');
    ['utm_source', 'utm_medium', 'utm_campaign', 'utm_term', 'utm_content', 'gclid', 'fbclid'].forEach(function (k) { if (q.get(k)) b.set(k, q.get(k)); });
    remember(city);
    return b;
  }
  window.__keGeo = {P: P, me: me, sec: sec, list: list, match: match, pageCity: pageCity, example: example, saved: saved, payload: payload};
})();

      <section class="ke-gate__story" id="ke-gate-story" aria-labelledby="ke-gate-sh">
        <img class="ke-gate__ava" src="https://www.rallytakesthetrain.com/s/v92177zaefz788c0aap0y70d1u6vu4" alt="Kelsey Lizee Cecil with Rally, the real corgi" width="64" height="64" loading="lazy">
        <div>
          <p class="ke-gate__kick" data-k="kick">Kelsey Lizee Cecil, author</p>
          <h3 class="ke-gate__sh" id="ke-gate-sh" data-k="sh">A book my mom dreamed up. I wrote it for her.</h3>
          <p class="ke-gate__sp" data-k="sp2">My mom, Debra Kay Lizee, passed away in 2021, in the middle of the pandemic, before she ever wrote a word of it. So I did. I wrote it so her idea would not end with her.</p>
          <details class="ke-gate__more" id="ke-gate-more"><summary data-k="more">Read the rest of my story</summary>
            <p class="ke-gate__sp" data-k="sp">I wrote Rally Takes the Train, but it was my mom&rsquo;s idea first. For as long as I can remember, she talked about a little corgi who packs his toys, rides the train along the coast with his family, and finds his grand-paw-rents waiting on the platform at the very last stop. Rally is our real corgi, and she adored him.</p>
            <p class="ke-gate__sp" data-k="sp3">I wrote the story in rhyme, the way she always described it, short enough to read at bedtime in about five minutes. It is a happy book. Her idea was a happy one, and the book had to be too.</p>
            <p class="ke-gate__sp" data-k="sp4">Leslie Dunn painted every page by hand in real watercolor on paper. Nothing was generated or retouched with AI, because it had to be made by people who cared about it.</p>
            <p class="ke-gate__sp" data-k="sp5">Mom, thank you for the perfect idea that let me channel my grief into something bigger. Dad, thank you for supporting my goal of making this book a reality in her honor. I&rsquo;m sorry you never got to see it completed, but this book is now for you too.</p>
            <p class="ke-gate__sp"><a class="ke-gate__osl" href="/our-story" data-k="osl">Read our whole story</a></p>
          </details>
        </div>

  function kgbInit(root) {

  if (!root || !('content' in document.createElement('template'))) return;

  var tpl   = root.querySelector('#kgb-pages');
  var book  = root.querySelector('[data-book]');
  var label = root.querySelector('[data-rtt-label]');
  var prevB = root.querySelector('[data-rtt-prev]');
  var nextB = root.querySelector('[data-rtt-next]');
  var mq = {matches: true};

  function page(name, side) {
    var el = tpl.content.querySelector('[data-page="' + name + '"]').cloneNode(true);
    if (side) el.classList.add('kgb-pg--' + side);
    return el;
  }
  function blank(side) {
    var f = document.createElement('figure');
    f.className = 'kgb-pg kgb-pg--blank' + (side ? ' kgb-pg--' + side : '');
    f.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
    return f;
  }
  function leaf(frontEl, backEl) {
    var l = document.createElement('div'); l.className = 'kgb-leaf';
    var f = document.createElement('div'); f.className = 'kgb-leaf__face kgb-leaf__front'; f.appendChild(frontEl);
    var b = document.createElement('div'); b.className = 'kgb-leaf__face kgb-leaf__back';  b.appendChild(backEl);
    l.appendChild(f); l.appendChild(b);
    return l;
  }

  var state = 0, leaves = [], labels = [], mode = null, busy = false;

  function build() {
    var m = mq.matches ? 'single' : 'spread';
    if (m === mode) return;
    mode = m;
    book.innerHTML = '';
    leaves = []; labels = [];

    var baseL = document.createElement('div'); baseL.className = 'kgb-book__base kgb-book__base--left';
    var baseR = document.createElement('div'); baseR.className = 'kgb-book__base kgb-book__base--right';

    if (mode === 'spread') {
      baseL.appendChild(page('title', 'left'));
      baseR.appendChild(blank('right'));
      leaves.push(leaf(page('1', 'right'), page('2', 'left')));
      leaves.push(leaf(page('3', 'right'), page('4', 'left')));
      leaves.push(leaf(page('5', 'right'), page('6', 'left')));
      leaves.push(leaf(page('7', 'right'), page('8', 'left')));
      leaves.push(leaf(page('cta', 'right'), blank('left')));
      labels = ['Page 1 of 8', 'Pages 2 to 3 of 8', 'Pages 4 to 5 of 8', 'Pages 6 to 7 of 8', 'Page 8 of 8'];
    } else {
      baseR.appendChild(blank('right'));
      ['1', '2', '3', '4', '5', '6', '7', '8', 'cta'].forEach(function (n) { leaves.push(leaf(page(n, 'right'), blank('left'))); });
      labels = ['Page 1 of 8', 'Page 2 of 8', 'Page 3 of 8', 'Page 4 of 8', 'Page 5 of 8', 'Page 6 of 8', 'Page 7 of 8', 'Page 8 of 8', 'The last stop'];
    }
    book.appendChild(baseL); book.appendChild(baseR);
    leaves.forEach(function (l) { book.appendChild(l); });

    state = Math.min(state, leaves.length - 1);
    render(true);
  }

  function render(instant) {
    leaves.forEach(function (l, i) {
      var flipped = i < state;
      if (instant) { l.style.transition = 'none'; }
      l.classList.toggle('is-flipped', flipped);
      l.classList.toggle('is-next', i === state);
      l.style.zIndex = flipped ? (10 + i) : (10 + leaves.length - i);
      if (instant) { void l.offsetWidth; l.style.transition = ''; }
    });
    label.textContent = KGLAB(state, labels);
    prevB.disabled = state === 0;
    nextB.disabled = state === leaves.length - 1;
    root.classList.toggle('is-turned', state >= 1);
  }

  /* ---- peel: slice the leaf into strips and bend them along a curve while it turns ---- */
  var STRIPS = 14, BEND = 38;            /* strip count, max bend in degrees at mid turn */
  var cast = document.createElement('div'); cast.className = 'kgb-peel__cast';

  function makeStrips(l, W, w) {
    var wrap = document.createElement('div'); wrap.className = 'kgb-peel';
    var frontPg = l.querySelector('.kgb-leaf__front .kgb-pg');
    var backPg  = l.querySelector('.kgb-leaf__back .kgb-pg');
    var strips = [];
    for (var i = 0; i < STRIPS; i++) {
      var pair = {};
      [['f', frontPg, -i * w], ['b', backPg, -(STRIPS - 1 - i) * w]].forEach(function (d) {
        var st = document.createElement('div'); st.className = 'kgb-peel__strip';
        st.style.width = (w + 1) + 'px';
        if (d[1]) {
          var c = d[1].cloneNode(true);
          c.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
          c.querySelectorAll('[id]').forEach(function (n) { n.removeAttribute('id'); });
          c.style.left = d[2] + 'px'; c.style.width = W + 'px';
          st.appendChild(c);
        }
        var sh = document.createElement('div'); sh.className = 'kgb-peel__shade'; st.appendChild(sh);
        wrap.appendChild(st);
        pair[d[0]] = st; pair[d[0] + 's'] = sh;
      });
      strips.push(pair);
    }
    l.appendChild(wrap);
    return { wrap: wrap, strips: strips };
  }

  function easeInOut(t) { return t < .5 ? 4 * t * t * t : 1 - Math.pow(-2 * t + 2, 3) / 2; }

  function peel(l, dir, dur, onDone) {
    var W = l.offsetWidth, w = W / STRIPS;
    var parts = makeStrips(l, W, w);
    l.classList.add('is-peeling');
    l.style.zIndex = 60;
    book.appendChild(cast);
    var t0 = null;

    function frame(now) {
      if (t0 === null) t0 = now;
      var p = Math.min(1, (now - t0) / dur);
      var q = dir > 0 ? easeInOut(p) : 1 - easeInOut(p);   /* 0 = flat on the right, 1 = flat on the left */
      var A = q * 180;
      var B = BEND * Math.sin(Math.PI * q) * dir;          /* free edge leads in the direction of travel */
      var x = 0, z = 0;
      for (var i = 0; i < STRIPS; i++) {
        var a = A + B * ((i + 1) / STRIPS);                 /* degrees */
        if (a < 0) a = 0; if (a > 180) a = 180;
        var rad = a * Math.PI / 180;
        var pos = 'translate3d(' + x.toFixed(2) + 'px,0,' + z.toFixed(2) + 'px) ';
        var st = parts.strips[i];
        st.f.style.transform = pos + 'rotateY(' + (-a).toFixed(3) + 'deg)';
        st.b.style.transform = pos + 'rotateY(' + (180 - a).toFixed(3) + 'deg) translateX(-100%)';
        var shade = Math.sin(rad);
        st.fs.style.opacity = (0.28 * shade).toFixed(3);
        st.bs.style.opacity = (0.28 * shade).toFixed(3);
        x += w * Math.cos(rad); z += w * Math.sin(rad);
      }
      /* shadow the leaf casts on the page beneath */
      var lift = Math.sin(A * Math.PI / 180);
      cast.style.opacity = (0.55 * lift).toFixed(3);
      cast.style.width = Math.max(0, Math.abs(x)) + 'px';
      cast.classList.toggle('is-left', x < 0);
      if (p < 1) { requestAnimationFrame(frame); } else { finish(); }
    }
    function finish() {
      parts.wrap.remove();
      cast.remove();
      l.classList.remove('is-peeling');
      onDone();
    }
    requestAnimationFrame(frame);
  }

  /* tell the rest of the page (the sticky Rally bar listens) which page just came up */
  function announce(dir) {
    var visible;
    if (mode === 'spread') {
      if (state === 0) visible = ['title'];
      else if (state === leaves.length - 1) visible = [String(2 * state), 'cta'];
      else visible = [String(2 * state), String(2 * state + 1)];
    } else {
      visible = state === leaves.length - 1 ? ['cta'] : [String(state + 1)];
    }
    var order = dir > 0 ? visible : visible.slice().reverse();
    var keys = order.map(function (name) {
      var fig = tpl.content.querySelector('[data-page="' + name + '"]');
      return name === 'cta' ? 'cta' : name === 'title' ? 'title' : (fig && fig.getAttribute('data-key')) || name;
    });
    try { kgbFlip(keys[0], dir, state, leaves.length); } catch (e) {}
  }

  function turn(dir) {
    if (busy) return;
    var next = state + dir;
    if (next < 0 || next > leaves.length - 1) return;
    var l = leaves[dir > 0 ? state : state - 1];
    var dur = parseFloat(getComputedStyle(root).getPropertyValue('--flip')) || 1000;
    busy = true;
    state = next;
    announce(dir);
    if (dur < 50 || !window.requestAnimationFrame) {   /* reduced motion: just switch */
      render(true); busy = false; return;
    }
    /* set the final state instantly underneath, then run the peel on top */
    l.style.transition = 'none';
    l.classList.toggle('is-flipped', dir > 0);
    void l.offsetWidth;
    label.textContent = KGLAB(state, labels);
    prevB.disabled = state === 0;
    nextB.disabled = state === leaves.length - 1;
    root.classList.toggle('is-turned', state >= 1);
    peel(l, dir, dur, function () {
      l.style.transition = '';
      render(true);
      busy = false;
    });
  }

  /* controls */
  prevB.addEventListener('click', function () { turn(-1); });
  nextB.addEventListener('click', function () { turn(1); });
  book.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
    if (e.key === 'ArrowRight') { e.preventDefault(); turn(1); }
    if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft')  { e.preventDefault(); turn(-1); }
  });
  /* click the right half to go forward, left half to go back (links still work) */
  book.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    if (e.target.closest('a')) return;
    var r = book.getBoundingClientRect();
    turn(e.clientX - r.left > r.width / 2 ? 1 : -1);
  });
  /* swipe */
  var sx = null, sy = null;
  book.addEventListener('touchstart', function (e) { sx = e.touches[0].clientX; sy = e.touches[0].clientY; }, { passive: true });
  book.addEventListener('touchend', function (e) {
    if (sx === null) return;
    var dx = e.changedTouches[0].clientX - sx, dy = e.changedTouches[0].clientY - sy;
    if (Math.abs(dx) > 40 && Math.abs(dx) > Math.abs(dy)) turn(dx < 0 ? 1 : -1);
    sx = sy = null;
  }, { passive: true });

  build();

  }

  }
  function KGLAB(i, labs) { if (!GX) return labs[i]; return i >= 8 ? GX.last : GX.page.replace('{n}', i + 1); }
  function kgbFlip(key, dir, idx, total) {
    GS.flips++; var n = idx + 1; if (n > GS.flipMax) GS.flipMax = Math.min(n, 8);
    track('ke_gate_flip', {page: key, page_number: n, direction: dir > 0 ? 'next' : 'back', book_flips: GS.flips});
    if (idx === total - 1) { track('ke_gate_flip_complete', {book_flips: GS.flips, time_open_s: secsOpen()}); ads(['trackCustom', 'BookPreviewComplete'], null, {}); }
  }

  function unlocked(){ var sk = null; try { sk = sessionStorage.getItem('ke-skip'); } catch (e) {} return !!get(KEY) || !!sk || document.documentElement.getAttribute('data-ke-gate') === 'off'; }
  function gated(a){
    if (!a || !a.href || a.closest('#ke-gate')) return false;
    if (EV ? !a.closest('.ke-gated') : !a.closest('main, #page, article')) return false;
    if (a.closest('header, footer, #header, #footer-sections, .header, [data-ke-skip]') || a.hasAttribute('download')) return false;
    if (MUS && (document.documentElement.classList.contains('rtt-kidmode') || a.closest('#rtt-kids, .rtt-kz'))) return false;
    var u; try { u = new URL(a.href, location.href); } catch (e) { return false; }
    if (!/^https?:$/.test(u.protocol)) return false;
    var h = u.hostname.replace(/^www\./, '');
    if (h === location.hostname.replace(/^www\./, '') || /(^|\.)rallytakesthetrain\.com$/.test(h)) return false;
    if (/^(maps\.google\.|google\.[a-z.]+$)/.test(h) && /\/maps/.test(u.pathname)) return false;
    if (a.closest('.rtt-cal') || /(^|\.)(calendar\.google\.com|outlook\.live\.com|outlook\.office\.com|outlook\.office365\.com|calendar\.yahoo\.com)$/.test(h)) return false; // add-to-calendar links are a tool, never gated (Nov 10, 2026)
    // never put a sign-up between a grieving reader and crisis or support help
    if (/(^|\.)(988lifeline\.org|suicidepreventionlifeline\.org|crisistextline\.org|samhsa\.gov|findtreatment\.gov|nami\.org|mentalhealth\.gov|988\.ca|talksuicide\.ca|samaritans\.org|lifeline\.org\.au|befrienders\.org|iasp\.info|findahelpline\.com|dougy\.org|childrengrieve\.org|compassionatefriends\.org|griefshare\.org)$/.test(h)) return false;
    if (document.querySelector('[class*="flal-"]') && /(crisis|988|hotline|helpline|lifeline|suicide|therap|counsel)/i.test((a.textContent || '') + ' ' + a.href)) return false;
    if (/(^|\.)(amazon\.com|amzn\.to|barnesandnoble\.com|bookshop\.org|apple\.com|kobo\.com|target\.com|walmart\.com|booksamillion\.com|docsend\.com|squarespace\.com|youtube\.com|instagram\.com|facebook\.com|tiktok\.com|threads\.com|threads\.net|pinterest\.com|schema\.org)$/.test(h)) return false;
    return true;
  }
  function open(a){
    pending = a; lastFocus = a; setIntro(a);

  }

        '</div>' +
        '<div class="rta__body">' +
          '<form class="rta__form" novalidate>' +
            '<div class="rta__f"><label for="kn-email">' + esc(T.lbl) + '</label><input id="kn-email" name="email" type="email" autocomplete="email" inputmode="email" autocapitalize="off" autocorrect="off" spellcheck="false" enterkeyhint="next" required aria-describedby="kn-err kn-fine" placeholder="' + esc(T.ph) + '"></div>' +
            '<div class="rta__f"><label for="kn-city">' + esc(T.clbl) + '</label><input id="kn-city" name="city" type="text" autocomplete="address-level2" autocapitalize="words" enterkeyhint="send" list="kn-cities" aria-describedby="kn-chint" placeholder="' + esc(T.cph + (ex && !cityVal ? ' (' + ex + ')' : '')) + '" value="' + esc(cityVal) + '"><datalist id="kn-cities">' + cities.map(function (n) { return '<option value="' + esc(n) + '">'; }).join('') + '</datalist><p class="kn__chint" id="kn-chint">' + esc(pageCity ? T.chintPre : T.chint) + '</p></div>' +

          '</form>' +

          '<div class="rta__share" role="group" aria-labelledby="kn-sh"><p class="rta__sh" id="kn-sh" role="heading" aria-level="3">' + esc(T.shH) + '</p><p class="rta__sp">' + esc(T.shP) + '</p>' +

            '<p class="rta__sok" id="kn-sok" role="status" aria-live="polite"></p></div>' +
        '</div>' +
      '</div>';
    return r;
  }
  function wire(root) {
    var card = root.querySelector('.rta__card'), form = root.querySelector('form'), email = root.querySelector('#kn-email'), err = root.querySelector('#kn-err'), go = root.querySelector('.rta__go'), sok = root.querySelector('#kn-sok');
    function fail(msg, field) { err.textContent = msg; err.hidden = false; if (field) { field.setAttribute('aria-invalid', 'true'); field.focus(); } }
    var cityIn = root.querySelector('#kn-city'); if (pageCity) cityIn.classList.add('is-pre'); cityIn.addEventListener('input', function () { cityIn.classList.remove('is-pre'); });
    email.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) { if (e.key === 'Enter' && !cityIn.value && /@/.test(email.value)) { e.preventDefault(); cityIn.focus(); } });
    form.addEventListener('submit', function (e) {
      e.preventDefault(); err.hidden = true; email.removeAttribute('aria-invalid');
      var em = email.value.trim();
      if (!/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]{2,}$/.test(em)) return fail(T.err, email);
      if (root.querySelector('#kn-co').value) { done(true); return; }
      var cv = root.querySelector('#kn-city').value.trim(), body;
      if (G) body = G.payload({email: em, city: cv, lang: L, country: C, state: (gd && gd.getAttribute('data-ke-state')) || '', source: 'notifications widget', list: EV ? 'christmas-events-for-kids-guide' : 'site-link-gate', friend: invited});
      else { body = new URLSearchParams(); body.set('email', em); body.set('city', cv); body.set('lang', L); body.set('country', C); body.set('page', path); body.set('page_url', location.href.split('#')[0]); body.set('subscribe', 'rally-blog'); body.set('source', 'notifications widget'); }
      go.disabled = true; go.lastChild.textContent = T.busy;
      var fired = false, ok = function () { if (fired) return; fired = true; go.disabled = false; go.lastChild.textContent = T.go; done(); };
      try {
        fetch(HOOK, {method: 'POST', mode: 'no-cors', body: body, keepalive: true}).then(ok, function () {
          var b = false; try { b = navigator.sendBeacon && navigator.sendBeacon(HOOK, body); } catch (x) {}
          if (b) ok(); else { fired = true; go.disabled = false; go.lastChild.textContent = T.go; fail(T.errNet); }
        });
      } catch (x) { ok(); }
    });
    function say(t) { sok.textContent = ''; setTimeout(function () { sok.textContent = t; }, 60); }
    function copyLink(btn, url) {
      var okc = function () { btn.lastChild.textContent = T.copied; say(T.copiedSay); ga('kn_share', {method: 'copy'}); };
      var old = function () { var t = document.createElement('textarea'); t.value = url; t.setAttribute('readonly', ''); t.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;top:0;opacity:0'; document.body.appendChild(t); t.select(); var s = false; try { s = document.execCommand('copy'); } catch (y) {} t.remove(); btn.focus(); if (s) okc(); else say(T.copyThis + url); };
      try { navigator.clipboard.writeText(url).then(okc, old); } catch (x) { old(); }
    }
    root.knCopy = copyLink; root.knSay = say;
    function doShare(btn) {
      if (navigator.share) navigator.share({title: document.title, text: shareText, url: shareUrl}).then(function () { ga('kn_share', {method: 'native'}); say(T.thanks); }, function (er) { if (!er || er.name !== 'AbortError') copyLink(btn, shareUrl); });
      else copyLink(btn, shareUrl);
    }
    // share image for Instagram and TikTok, drawn in the visitor's browser
    var imgApp = 'Instagram', imgFmt = 'story', imgFile = null, imgUrl = '', artP = null, renderN = 0, canFiles = false;
    try { canFiles = !!(navigator.canShare && navigator.canShare({files: [new File(['x'], 'x.png', {type: 'image/png'})]})); } catch (x) {}
    var panel = root.querySelector('#kn-img'), prev = root.querySelector('.rta__prev'), goImg = root.querySelector('[data-kn-go]'), tip = root.querySelector('.rta__tip');
    function art() { if (!artP) artP = new Promise(function (res) { var im = new Image(); im.crossOrigin = 'anonymous'; im.onload = function () { res(im); }; im.onerror = function () { res(null); }; im.src = 'https://res.cloudinary.com/hxoymedx/image/upload/w_700/f_auto,q_auto/v1791955306/Rally_Takes_The_Train_Christmas.png'; }); return artP; }
    function rr(c, x, y, w, h, r) { c.beginPath(); c.moveTo(x + r, y); c.arcTo(x + w, y, x + w, y + h, r); c.arcTo(x + w, y + h, x, y + h, r); c.arcTo(x, y + h, x, y, r); c.arcTo(x, y, x + w, y, r); c.closePath(); }
    var JF = L === 'ja' ? ', "Hiragino Sans", "Noto Sans JP", "Yu Gothic", sans-serif' : '';
    function tx(c, t, x, y, size, font, color, ls, maxW) {
      var s = size; c.font = font.replace('#', s) + JF; if ('letterSpacing' in c) c.letterSpacing = (ls || 0) + 'px';
      while (maxW && c.measureText(t).width > maxW && s > 12) { s -= 2; c.font = font.replace('#', s) + JF; }
      c.fillStyle = color; c.fillText(t, x, y); if ('letterSpacing' in c) c.letterSpacing = '0px';
    }
    function makeImg(fmt) {
      var W = 1080, H = fmt === 'story' ? 1920 : 1350, s = fmt === 'story' ? 1 : .78;
      var fl = ['600 100px Fraunces', 'italic 500 100px Fraunces', '700 40px Nunito', '600 40px Nunito'].map(function (f) { try { return document.fonts.load(f).catch(function () {}); } catch (x) { return null; } });
      return Promise.all([art()].concat(fl)).then(function (r) {
        var im = r[0], cv = document.createElement('canvas'); cv.width = W; cv.height = H; var c = cv.getContext('2d'), MW = W - 120;
        var g = c.createRadialGradient(W / 2, -100, 60, W / 2, H * .35, H); g.addColorStop(0, '#2b4166'); g.addColorStop(.65, '#172235'); g.addColorStop(1, '#0e1626'); c.fillStyle = g; c.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
        var sd = 11, rnd = function () { sd = (sd * 9301 + 49297) % 233280; return sd / 233280; };
        for (var j = 0; j < 140; j++) { c.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,' + (.35 + rnd() * .5).toFixed(2) + ')'; c.beginPath(); c.arc(rnd() * W, rnd() * H, rnd() * 4.5 + 1.2, 0, 6.3); c.fill(); }
        var cols = ['#ee7b6c', '#f2c14e', '#5a9e74', '#7fb3d5', '#e05d7a'], segs = 4, sw = W / segs, top = 30 * s, sag = 70 * s, n = 0;
        c.strokeStyle = 'rgba(160,150,140,.7)'; c.lineWidth = 3;
        for (var q = 0; q < segs; q++) {
          var x0 = q * sw, x1 = x0 + sw; c.beginPath(); c.moveTo(x0, top); c.quadraticCurveTo(x0 + sw / 2, top + sag * 2, x1, top); c.stroke();
          for (var k = 1; k <= 4; k++) { var t = k / 5, bx = (1 - t) * (1 - t) * x0 + 2 * (1 - t) * t * (x0 + sw / 2) + t * t * x1, byy = (1 - t) * (1 - t) * top + 2 * (1 - t) * t * (top + sag * 2) + t * t * top;
            c.save(); c.translate(bx, byy); c.fillStyle = '#6b5d52'; c.fillRect(-6 * s, -2, 12 * s, 12 * s); c.shadowColor = cols[n % 5]; c.shadowBlur = 30; c.fillStyle = cols[n % 5]; c.beginPath(); c.ellipse(0, 28 * s, 13 * s, 20 * s, 0, 0, 6.3); c.fill(); c.restore(); n++; }
        }
        c.textAlign = 'center'; var y = 430 * s;
        tx(c, place ? place.toUpperCase() : T.iK, W / 2, y, Math.round(34 * s), '700 #px Nunito, sans-serif', '#f2c14e', 4 * s, MW);
        tx(c, T.iH1, W / 2, y + 125 * s, Math.round(108 * s), '600 #px Fraunces, Georgia, serif', '#fbf5ea', 0, MW);
        tx(c, T.iH2, W / 2, y + 245 * s, Math.round(116 * s), 'italic 500 #px Fraunces, Georgia, serif', '#ff9d8f', 0, MW);
        var cy = y + 520 * s, R = 215 * s;
        c.save(); c.beginPath(); c.arc(W / 2, cy, R + 14 * s, 0, 6.3); c.fillStyle = '#ee7b6c'; c.fill(); c.beginPath(); c.arc(W / 2, cy, R, 0, 6.3); c.fillStyle = '#fbf5ea'; c.fill(); c.clip();
        if (im) { var kk = Math.max(2 * R / im.width, 2 * R / im.height); c.drawImage(im, W / 2 - im.width * kk / 2, cy - im.height * kk / 2, im.width * kk, im.height * kk); }
        c.restore();
        var tw = 820 * s, th = 210 * s, tX = (W - tw) / 2, tY = cy + R + 70 * s, TW = tw - 100 * s;
        c.save(); c.translate(W / 2, tY + th / 2); c.rotate(-.025); c.translate(-W / 2, -(tY + th / 2));
        c.save(); c.shadowColor = 'rgba(0,0,0,.45)'; c.shadowBlur = 30; c.shadowOffsetY = 14; rr(c, tX, tY, tw, th, 26 * s); c.fillStyle = '#fbf5ea'; c.fill(); c.restore();
        c.textAlign = 'left';
        tx(c, T.tk.toUpperCase(), tX + 50 * s, tY + 62 * s, Math.round(26 * s), '700 #px Nunito, sans-serif', '#b8443a', 5 * s, TW);
        tx(c, fill(T.tt, V), tX + 50 * s, tY + 125 * s, Math.round(54 * s), '600 #px Fraunces, Georgia, serif', '#2b2622', 0, TW);
        tx(c, T.where + ': ' + (place || T.near), tX + 50 * s, tY + 175 * s, Math.round(32 * s), '600 #px Nunito, sans-serif', '#5f5650', 0, tw - 360 * s);
        c.save(); c.translate(tX + tw - 140 * s, tY + th - 28 * s); c.rotate(-.12); c.lineWidth = 6 * s; c.strokeStyle = '#2f6b4f'; c.fillStyle = 'rgba(251,245,234,.95)'; rr(c, -142 * s, -36 * s, 284 * s, 72 * s, 14 * s); c.fill(); c.stroke(); c.textAlign = 'center';
        tx(c, T.stamp.toUpperCase(), 0, 12 * s, Math.round(31 * s), '700 #px Nunito, sans-serif', '#2f6b4f', 3 * s, 250 * s); c.restore();
        c.restore();
        c.textAlign = 'center'; var by = tY + th + (fmt === 'story' ? 160 : 95) * s;
        tx(c, T.iSub, W / 2, by, Math.round(42 * s), '600 #px Nunito, sans-serif', '#d3dbe8', 0, MW);
        c.font = '700 ' + Math.round(44 * s) + 'px Nunito, sans-serif'; var pw = c.measureText('rallytakesthetrain.com').width + 96 * s;
        rr(c, (W - pw) / 2, by + 40 * s, pw, 96 * s, 48 * s); c.fillStyle = '#ee7b6c'; c.fill();
        tx(c, 'rallytakesthetrain.com', W / 2, by + 104 * s, Math.round(44 * s), '700 #px Nunito, sans-serif', '#2b2622');
        return new Promise(function (res, rej) { try { cv.toBlob(function (b) { b ? res(b) : rej(); }, 'image/png'); } catch (x) { rej(x); } });
      });
    }
    function renderImg() {
      var n = ++renderN; imgFile = null; goImg.disabled = true; goImg.lastChild.textContent = T.making;
      makeImg(imgFmt).then(function (b) {
        if (n !== renderN) return;
        imgFile = new File([b], 'rally-christmas-events-' + (slug || 'kids') + '-' + imgFmt + '.png', {type: 'image/png'});
        if (imgUrl) URL.revokeObjectURL(imgUrl); imgUrl = URL.createObjectURL(b); prev.src = imgUrl;
        prev.alt = fill(T.alt, {fmt: imgFmt === 'story' ? T.story : T.post});
        var A = {app: imgApp}; goImg.disabled = false; goImg.lastChild.textContent = canFiles ? fill(T.shareTo, A) : T.save;
        tip.textContent = fill(canFiles ? T.tipShare : T.tipSave, A); say(fill(T.ready, A));
      }, function () { if (n === renderN) { goImg.lastChild.textContent = T.noImg; say(T.noImgSay); } });
    }
    function openImg(btn) {
      imgApp = btn.getAttribute('data-kn-img') === 'tiktok' ? 'TikTok' : 'Instagram';
      [].forEach.call(root.querySelectorAll('[data-kn-img]'), function (b) { b.setAttribute('aria-expanded', b === btn ? 'true' : 'false'); });
      root.querySelector('#kn-imgh').textContent = fill(T.imgH, {app: imgApp}); panel.hidden = false; renderImg();
      var f = panel.querySelector('[aria-pressed="true"]'); if (f) f.focus({preventScroll: true});
    }
    function setFmt(f) { if (f === imgFmt) return; imgFmt = f; [].forEach.call(panel.querySelectorAll('[data-kn-fmt]'), function (b) { b.setAttribute('aria-pressed', b.getAttribute('data-kn-fmt') === f ? 'true' : 'false'); }); renderImg(); }
    function shareImg() {
      if (!imgFile) return;
      try { navigator.clipboard.writeText(shareUrl).catch(function () {}); } catch (x) {}
      var save = function () { var a = document.createElement('a'); a.href = imgUrl; a.download = imgFile.name; document.body.appendChild(a); a.click(); a.remove(); ga('kn_share', {method: imgApp.toLowerCase() + '_' + imgFmt + '_save'}); say(T.saved); };
      if (canFiles) navigator.share({files: [imgFile], title: document.title}).then(function () { ga('kn_share', {method: imgApp.toLowerCase() + '_' + imgFmt}); say(T.shared); }, function (er) { if (!er || er.name !== 'AbortError') save(); });
      else save();
    }
    function done(quiet) {
      set(KEY, 'email'); set(SUB, '1');
      if (!quiet) { ga('kn_signup', {kn_state: (gd && gd.getAttribute('data-ke-state')) || ''}); ga('generate_lead', {lead_source: 'christmas_notifications_widget'}); try { if (window.fbq) window.fbq('track', 'Lead', {content_name: 'Christmas notifications widget'}); } catch (x) {} }
      markDone(); art();
      var h = root.querySelector('.rta__done h3'); if (h) { h.setAttribute('tabindex', '-1'); h.focus({preventScroll: true}); }
    }
    root.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
      var ib = e.target.closest('[data-kn-img]'); if (ib) { openImg(ib); return; }
      var fb = e.target.closest('[data-kn-fmt]'); if (fb) { setFmt(fb.getAttribute('data-kn-fmt')); return; }
      if (e.target.closest('[data-kn-go]')) { shareImg(); return; }
      var sb = e.target.closest('[data-kn-share]'); if (sb) { doShare(sb); return; }
      var so = e.target.closest('a.rta__so'); if (so) ga('kn_share', {method: so.getAttribute('data-kn-m')});
    });
  }
  // after an email from this widget or the link pop-up, every widget and event button switches to sharing
  function markDone() {
    if (widget) widget.classList.add('is-done');
    [].forEach.call(document.querySelectorAll('.kn-jump'), setJump);
  }
  function setJump(b) {
    var on = subbed(), n = b.getAttribute('data-kn-name') || '';
    b.setAttribute('data-kn-mode', on ? 'share' : 'notify');
    b.innerHTML = I(on ? SHARE_I : BELL) + '<span><b>' + esc(on ? T.jbS : T.jb) + '</b><small>' + esc(on ? T.jsS : T.js) + '</small></span>';
    b.setAttribute('aria-label', on ? fill(T.jAriaS, {n: n || document.title}) : T.jAria);
  }
  function jump(name, id, card) {
    var b = document.createElement('button'); b.type = 'button'; b.className = 'rta-jump kn-jump' + (card ? ' rta-jump--card' : '');
    b.setAttribute('data-kn-name', name || ''); b.setAttribute('data-kn-id', id || ''); setJump(b); return b;
  }
  function addButtons(scope) {
    [].forEach.call((scope || document).querySelectorAll('.rtt-pe-ride__book'), function (a) { var ride = a.closest('.rtt-pe-ride'); if (!ride || ride.querySelector('.kn-jump')) return; var h = ride.querySelector('h3, h2'); a.parentNode.insertBefore(jump(h ? h.textContent.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim() : '', ride.id), a.nextSibling); });
    [].forEach.call((scope || document).querySelectorAll('.xm-card__act'), function (f) { var c = f.closest('.xm-card'); if (!c || c.querySelector('.kn-jump')) return; var h = c.querySelector('h3'); f.appendChild(jump(h ? h.textContent.trim() : '', c.id, true)); });
  }
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var b = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('.kn-jump'); if (!b) return;
    if (b.getAttribute('data-kn-mode') === 'share') {
      var id = b.getAttribute('data-kn-id'), name = b.getAttribute('data-kn-name'), url = location.origin + path + (id ? '#' + id : '');
      var t = fill(T.evText, {n: name || document.title});
      ga('kn_event_share', {event_name: name});
      var lb = {lastChild: b.querySelector('b'), focus: function () { b.focus(); }};
      if (navigator.share) navigator.share({title: name || document.title, text: t, url: url}).catch(function (er) { if (er && er.name !== 'AbortError' && widget) widget.knCopy(lb, url); });
      else if (widget) widget.knCopy(lb, url);
      return;
    }
    if (!widget) return;
    ga('kn_jump', {event_name: b.getAttribute('data-kn-name') || ''});
    var reduce = !!(window.matchMedia && matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches);
    widget.scrollIntoView({behavior: reduce ? 'auto' : 'smooth', block: 'center'});
    widget.classList.add('is-flash'); setTimeout(function () { widget.classList.remove('is-flash'); }, 1800);
    setTimeout(function () { var f = widget.querySelector('input[type="email"]'); if (f) f.focus({preventScroll: true}); }, reduce ? 50 : 650);
  });
  document.addEventListener('ke:unlocked', function () { if (subbed()) markDone(); });
  function topBlock(el, box) { while (el && el.parentNode !== box) el = el.parentNode; return el; }
  function mount() {
    if (made) return true;
    var w = build(), at = null, before = null, wrap = false;
    var post = [].filter.call(document.querySelectorAll('.rtt-post'), function (p) { return p.querySelector('.rtt-post__tldr'); })[0];
    var lp = document.querySelector('article.rtt-lp');
    if (post) { var tl = topBlock(post.querySelector('.rtt-post__tldr'), post); at = post; before = tl.nextSibling; }
    else return false;
    if (!at) return false;
    made = true; widget = w;
    var node = w;
    if (wrap) { node = document.createElement('div'); node.className = 'kn-wrap'; node.appendChild(w); }
    at.insertBefore(node, before || null);
    wire(w); if (subbed()) w.classList.add('is-done');
    if (EV) {
      addButtons();
      [].forEach.call(document.querySelectorAll('#xm-grid'), function (g) { if ('MutationObserver' in window) new MutationObserver(function () { addButtons(g); }).observe(g, {childList: true, subtree: true}); });
    }
    if ('IntersectionObserver' in window) { var seen = false, io = new IntersectionObserver(function (es) { es.forEach(function (e) { if (e.isIntersecting && !seen) { seen = true; ga('kn_view', {returning: subbed() ? 'yes' : 'no'}); io.disconnect(); } }); }, {threshold: .35}); io.observe(w); }
    if (invited) setTimeout(function () { w.classList.add('is-invited'); var reduce = !!(window.matchMedia && matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches); w.scrollIntoView({behavior: reduce ? 'auto' : 'smooth', block: 'start'}); }, 700);
    return true;
  }
  if (!mount()) { if (document.readyState === 'loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', mount); else setTimeout(mount, 400); }
})();

</script>

  function goEv(){

  function goTr(){

    var img=[].filter.call(root.querySelectorAll('img'),function(i){return /Rally_Takes_The_Christmas_Train/.test(i.getAttribute('src')||i.getAttribute('data-src')||'');})[0];
    var fig=img&&(img.closest('figure')||img);
    if(root.classList.contains('rtt-lp')){
      var hero=root.querySelector('.rtt-lp__hero');
      if(hero) hero.parentNode.insertBefore(box,hero.nextSibling); else root.insertBefore(box,root.firstChild);
    } else if(fig){
      fig.parentNode.replaceChild(box,fig);
    } else {
      var toc=root.querySelector('.rtt-post__toc'), tl=root.querySelector('.rtt-post__tldr');
      if(toc) toc.parentNode.insertBefore(box,toc);
      else if(tl) tl.parentNode.insertBefore(box,tl.nextSibling);
      else return;
    }
    build(box,lang,'','trains');
  }

},false);
function hook(){
[].forEach.call(document.querySelectorAll('iframe'),function(f){
var d; try{d=f.contentDocument;}catch(_){return;}
if(!d||d.__rttsub) return; d.__rttsub=1;
d.addEventListener('click',function(e){
var a=e.target&&e.target.closest&&e.target.closest(SEL);
if(a) open(a,e,'tracker');
},false);
});
}
hook(); setInterval(hook,1500);
})();
